Conclusion: 显式序列标识与结构化推理监督结合时空奖励的强化学习，可显著提升长时序遥感理解能力，且不牺牲通用遥感任务表现。

Abstract: Long-horizon Earth observation reasoning requires models to organize multi-stage geographic evolution, localize spatial changes, detect temporal anomalies, and infer future from extended image sequences. However, existing remote sensing vision-language models mainly focus on isolated images, image pairs, or short sequences, limiting reliable grounding in the relevant frames and regions. We introduce LongEarth-Bench, a benchmark containing approximately 120k question-answering samples derived from 117k unique images. Its sequences average 15.14 frames and extend to 30 frames, covering 12 tasks across evolution summarization, spatial reasoning, anomaly identification, and logical prediction. A 30k-sample subset further provides structured reasoning traces linking key frames and changed regions to final answers. We develop LongEarth through supervised fine-tuning with explicit sequence identifiers and structured chain-of-thought supervision. Building on LongEarth, LongEarth-R1 applies group relative policy optimization with format, temporal, and spatial rewards. LongEarth-R1 achieves the best results on all 12 long-sequence tasks while remaining competitive on standard remote sensing benchmarks.

</details>


### [145] [Who Speaks Matters: Authority-Aware Multi-View RAG over Italian Parliamentary Proceedings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13410)
*Mirko Tritella,Riccardo Pozzi,Matteo Palmonari*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出ParliamentRAG，一种针对意大利众议院会议记录的检索增强生成系统，通过主题相关的权威模型解决发言者主导、专业权重和引用归因问题，在覆盖率和引用忠实度上优于NotebookLM，但散文质量略逊。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 议会记录对公民、记者和研究者难以多视角访问，现有RAG在议会场景中面临三大风险：频繁发言者主导、无法按专题专长加权、政治敏感文本中的引用错误归因。

Method: ParliamentRAG核心是主题相关的权威模型，按查询估计发言者权威度（结合职业、教育、历史发言等可解释组件），检索相关发言片段，识别跨党派的主题专家，生成综合多方视角的摘要并附支持性引文。

Result: 在15个政策主题上对照Google NotebookLM，ParliamentRAG在政治群体覆盖率（0.97 vs 0.95）、引用忠实度（1.00 vs 0.95）及专家对来源维度的偏好上更强，但NotebookLM在散文导向维度更优。

Conclusion: ParliamentRAG能更均衡、可信地呈现议会多视角内容，但需在文本流畅性上继续改进，以全面超越通用工具。

Abstract: Parliamentary proceedings are a primary record of democratic deliberation, yet their volume and fragmentation make multi-perspective access difficult for citizens, journalists, and researchers. Applying Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) to parliamentary transcripts introduces three specific risks: dominance of the most frequent speakers, inability to weight speakers according to topical expertise, and citation misattribution in politically sensitive text. We present ParliamentRAG, a RAG system for the Italian Chamber of Deputies that addresses these risks jointly. Its core contribution is a topic-dependent authority model that estimates each speaker's authority as a function of the current query, combining interpretable components such as profession, education, and previous interventions. Given a user query, the system retrieves relevant speech chunks, identifies topic-relevant experts across parliamentary groups, and generates a summary synthesizing their perspectives, accompanied by supporting quotations. ParliamentRAG is evaluated against Google NotebookLM on 15 policy topics via a two-level protocol combining automated metrics and blind A/B human evaluation by six domain experts. The system achieves higher coverage across political groups (0.97 vs. 0.95), perfect quotation faithfulness (1.00 vs. 0.95), and stronger expert preferences on source-related dimensions, while NotebookLM remains stronger on prose-oriented dimensions.

</details>


### [146] [Simulation-to-real transfer learning for infrared spectroscopic chemical sensing and analysis from molecules to complex samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13341)
*Yusen Tan,Yixuan Chen,Zheng Fang,Pan Liu,Yifan Li,Qinyu Guo,Zhedong Lin,Yuqiang Li,Xiangxiang Zeng,Tong Wang,Jun Xia*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: UltraIR是一个拥有超1亿参数的红外光谱基础模型，通过约6000万模拟光谱预训练，实现从分子到复杂样品的仿真到真实迁移学习，在多个分析任务中优于传统机器学习和任务特定深度学习基线，支持零样本跨仪器泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统红外光谱解析依赖人工经验和参考谱库，劳动密集且难以扩展；现有机器学习方法局限于特定任务或数据集，需要大量标注样本且跨目标和数据集迁移能力差，亟需通用、数据高效的分析工具。

Method: 模型基于约6000万模拟红外光谱预训练，联合优化光谱重建、分子指纹相似性对齐和官能团预测三个目标，随后通过任务特定标签或目标微调适配下游任务，实现仿真到真实数据的迁移。

Result: 在官能团预测、分子结构解析、理化性质预测、混合物组分识别与定量、细菌分类、药材产地溯源与成分定量、微塑料分类及土壤性质预测等任务中均超越传统机器学习和任务特定深度学习基线，且在有限标注数据和零样本跨谱仪/实验室场景下表现稳健。

Conclusion: UltraIR为红外光谱分析提供了一条自适应、数据高效的技术路径，显著提升复杂真实世界样品化学传感的泛化能力和实用性。

Abstract: Infrared (IR) spectroscopy is widely used for chemical sensing, but extracting reliable chemical information from spectra remains challenging. Conventional interpretation is labor-intensive, relies on prior knowledge and reference spectra, and is difficult to scale, whereas most machine-learning methods are tailored to individual tasks or datasets, require large labeled training sets, and transfer poorly across analytical objectives and experimental datasets. Here we introduce UltraIR, a foundation model for IR spectroscopy with more than 100 million parameters that enables simulation-to-real transfer learning for chemical sensing and analysis from molecules to complex samples. UltraIR is pretrained on approximately 60 million simulated IR spectra using spectral reconstruction, molecular fingerprint similarity alignment, and functional-group prediction, then adapted to downstream objectives with task-specific labels or targets. Across functional-group prediction, molecular structure elucidation, physicochemical property prediction, mixture-component identification and quantification, bacterial classification, medicinal-herb geographic origin traceability and constituent quantification, microplastics classification, and soil property prediction, UltraIR outperforms conventional machine-learning and task-specific deep-learning baselines. It performs strongly with limited labeled experimental spectra and in zero-shot inference for the same analytical task across Fourier-transform infrared spectrometers and laboratories, providing a route to adaptable, data-efficient chemical sensing from complex real-world samples.

</details>


### [147] [Beyond Final Scores: A Systematic Evaluation of Agents for Long-Horizon AI Research and Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13417)
*Yiwei Li,Wanli Yang,Hexiang Tan,Xiangzhou Huang,Zhengyu Chen,Ziran Li,Borun Chen,Shanglin Lei,Huaisheng Zhu,Hao Tian,Fei Sun,Xunliang Cai,Jingang Wang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一个系统评估框架，通过规则指标和受控比较分析七种前沿模型在36个长时程任务上的行为，发现当前智能体更像工程优化器而非完全自主的研究者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估只看最终分数，无法揭示进展得失或经验积累的影响，需要更细粒度的过程性评估。

Method: 用规则指标刻画运行内行为（问题框架、执行、反馈控制），并通过受控比较评估任务内及跨任务的经验复用。

Result: 模型能制定并实施实用方案，但运行间表现波动大；最强方案多为改编或组合现有技术，真正的方法创新罕见；经验复用有时有帮助有时误导，且过程瓶颈和设计影响稳定性。

Conclusion: 提出改进模型训练、推理策略、经验管理和框架设计的具体方向，以推动自主智能体能力提升。

Abstract: Autonomous agents are increasingly capable of improving models, systems, and other technical artifacts through long-horizon experimentation. To understand the current state of this capability, however, evaluation must go beyond final scores, which neither reveal where progress is gained or lost nor indicate whether accumulated experience improves later decisions. We therefore present a systematic evaluation of seven frontier models on 36 long-horizon tasks based on a new framework that uses rule-based metrics to characterize within-run behavior through Solution Framing, Execution, and Feedback Control and controlled comparisons to assess experience reuse within and across tasks. The results show that current agents operate more like engineering optimizers than fully autonomous researchers: they can formulate and implement practical solutions, but their performance varies substantially across runs, their strongest solutions mainly adapt or combine established techniques, and genuine methodological novelty remains rare. Detailed analysis reveals that observed performance is shaped by multiple factors, including distinct process bottlenecks behind similar final outcomes, experience reuse that can help or mislead subsequent decisions, and harness designs that affect performance stability. These findings suggest concrete directions for improving model training, inference-time strategies, experience management, and harness design.

</details>


### [148] [Enhancing Virtual Agents through SLMs and Edge-Computing: An Exploratory Evaluation of Think and Memory Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13420)
*Aimilios Hadjiliasi,Louis Nisiotis*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文探讨在边缘计算环境中使用小语言模型（SLMs）实现认知具身智能体架构（CEAA）中的“思考”与“记忆”组件，并在NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX上验证了基于Qwen2.5的网关系统性能，表明SLM可支持虚拟世界中的边缘端认知操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能虚拟代理需具备持久性、适应性和情境感知能力，但在复杂虚拟世界中实现认知功能面临技术和概念挑战；边缘计算与生成式AI的进展为在资源受限设备上部署认知组件提供了新可能。

Method: 基于CEAA框架，聚焦其“思考”与“记忆”过程，设计并实现了一个边缘虚拟代理网关系统，使用不同尺寸的Qwen2.5模型在NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX上运行，并通过模拟实验评估路由准确性、记忆读取性能和延迟。

Result: 实验表明系统能有效处理服务请求和记忆驱动对话，在路由精度、记忆读取及延迟方面均表现出可行性能，验证了SLM驱动原型代理对CEAA部分过程的支持能力。

Conclusion: SLMs能在边缘设备上支撑具身代理的核心认知组件，为构建在沉浸式虚拟世界中高效、情境化运行的智能代理提供了一种可实现的技术路径。

Abstract: Embodied intelligent virtual agents are expected to operate as persistent, adaptive, and context-aware entities within complex virtual and Metaverse worlds. However, implementing cognitively capable agents in such environments is conceptually and technologically challenging. Among a range of blueprints and development approaches, the Cognitive Embodied Agent Architecture (CEAA) has been developed as an implementation-oriented framework for architecting components of perception, memory, reasoning, planning, and embodied action. Considering the recent advances in edge computing and generative AI language models, this paper explores the use of Small Language Models (SLMs) to support edge-based operation of selected CEAA components, focusing on "Think" and "Memory" as processes central to cognitive orchestration and persistence of virtual agents in interactive virtual worlds. An edge-based virtual agent gateway system was developed and evaluated on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX using Qwen2.5 models of different sizes, exploring the system's capability to process service requests and handle memory-driven conversations. A series of simulation experiments evaluated routing accuracy, memory-read performance, and latency, demonstrating an SLM-driven prototype agent system that partially implements selected CEAA processes to support the development of embodied agents whose cognitive "brain" can operate efficiently and contextually for interactive experiences in immersive virtual worlds.

</details>


### [149] [Reduced Matrix Multiplication: Input-Adaptive Matrix-Product Reduction for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13426)
*Zixuan Lan,Yanhong Li,Jiawei Zhou*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出无需训练的输入自适应推理方法RMM，通过沿收缩维度选择信息性切片减少Transformer矩阵乘法，在多种模型和任务上实现平滑的精度-效率权衡，并能转化为实际运行加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer模型推理成本高，源于重复的高维矩阵乘法，现有方法通常需要训练或修改权重，缺乏简单有效的推理时优化手段。

Method: RMM在推理时根据输入动态选择矩阵乘法收缩维度上的信息性切片，保留比例可控，不修改模型权重，并支持自定义内核加速。

Result: 在1B到70B参数模型上验证，缩减容限因模型族、任务、组件和保留率而异，通常随规模提升；注意力侧比MLP更可缩减；多模态场景同样适用，长序列下A100上获得实际加速。

Conclusion: RMM提供了一种可扩展的输入自适应推理优化方向，能保持鲁棒性并带来实用收益。

Abstract: Transformer-based language models achieve strong performance but incur substantial inference cost due to repeated high-dimensional matrix multiplications. We propose Reduced Matrix Multiplication (RMM), a training-free, input-adaptive inference method that reduces Transformer matrix products by selecting informative slices along their contraction dimensions, without modifying model weights. Under a simple retention-ratio control, RMM provides a smooth and predictable accuracy-efficiency trade-off. Across language models ranging from 1B to 70B parameters, we find that reduction tolerance depends on the model family, task, component, and retention ratio, although it often improves with model scale. Under moderate reduction, RMM remains robust across the evaluated discriminative, autoregressive generation, and long-context settings. We further show that the same principle extends to multimodal vision-language inference. Mechanistic ablations reveal a structural asymmetry within Transformers: attention-side computations are substantially more reducible than MLP components. Finally, wall-clock benchmarks with custom kernels on an NVIDIA A100 show that these computational savings can translate into practical runtime gains, especially at longer sequence lengths. Together, these results position RMM as a scalable direction for input-adaptive inference-time optimization.

</details>


### [150] [Symmetry-Breaking De Novo Crystal Generation via Markovian Jump Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13457)
*Van Khoa Nguyen,Alexandros Kalousis*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一种基于自发对称破缺的扩散生成框架SbCD，通过马尔可夫跳跃-扩散过程从最低对称性先验逆向生成完整晶体结构，并在MP20和MPTS-52数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有晶体生成模型只能生成到位点对称性级别，空间群依赖经验分布采样，无法捕捉全局对称性和结构依赖关系，限制了生成晶体的完整性和物理合理性。

Method: 受物理中自发对称破缺启发，设计马尔可夫跳跃-扩散过程建模空间群之间的对称性破缺动力学，从最低对称性先验出发逆向生成完整晶体规格，显式融入空间群间转换。

Result: 在MP20和MPTS-52的从头生成实验中，SbCD相比保持对称性的基准方法取得了大幅性能提升。

Conclusion: SbCD为晶体材料生成提供了有前景的新视角，展示了显式建模空间群转换对生成质量的有效性。

Abstract: Generating crystals has recently attracted significant interest due to their broad applications in materials science. However, existing generative models struggle to produce complete crystallographic specifications, limiting their ability to capture global symmetry and structural dependencies. In particular, current state-of-the-art approaches generate crystals only up to site symmetries and rely on sampling space groups from empirical distributions during generation. Inspired by \emph{spontaneous symmetry breaking} in physics, where crystals break symmetries under external conditions, we propose a novel diffusion-based framework that generates full structure specifications by reversing from the lowest-symmetry priors. Our method leverages a Markovian jump-diffusion process to model these symmetry-breaking dynamics, enabling it to traverse different space groups in a physically motivated manner. Our model, dubbed \emph{Symmetry-breaking Crystal Diffusion} (SbCD), introduces a principled approach to explicitly incorporate inter-space-group transitions into the generative process. In de novo generation experiments on MP20 and MPTS-52, SbCD outperforms its symmetry-preserving counterpart by a substantial margin, offering a promising perspective for generative modeling of crystalline materials.

</details>


### [151] [A Unifying Perspective on Causal World Models: From Observations to Representations to Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13456)
*Avinash Kori,Fabrizio Russo*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文从因果视角系统研究世界模型，提出因果世界模型（CWM）的正式定义，强调其需超越生成能力，捕捉实体属性、实体间及实体与环境间交互，并连接因果表示学习、对象中心学习等领域，明确可识别性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型主要关注生成能力，缺乏对系统动态背后因果结构的显式建模，难以支持分布外预测、规划与决策。

Method: 通过多层级抽象分析，形式化定义CWM，将其与因果表示学习、对象中心学习、因果发现、结构因果模型及基于模型的决策制定联系起来，并讨论可识别性。

Result: 建立了CWM的理论框架，澄清了其组件从数据中恢复的条件与等价性类别，为因果推理和决策提供了支撑。

Conclusion: CWM为世界模型提供了因果基础，强调结构和交互建模，推动智能体具备更强的推理与决策能力。

Abstract: World Models (WM) are increasingly seen as a foundation for intelligent agents that can predict, plan, and act beyond their training distribution. In this paper, we study WMs from a causal perspective across multiple levels of abstraction, ranging from perceptual observations to building a conceptual representation of the structure governing the environment dynamics. We argue that useful WMs must go beyond generative capabilities alone: they should also capture entity properties, entity-to-entity interactions, and entity-to-environment interactions that determine and explain the dynamics of a system. We provide a formal definition of Causal WMs (CWMs) grounded in the tasks they are intended to support, connecting world modelling with existing work in causal representation learning, object-centric learning, causal discovery, structural causal models, and model-based decision-making. Finally, we relate CWMs to the literature on identifiability, clarifying when the components of a WM can be recovered from data and up to which equivalence. With this, we ground WMs in representations and structures that support causal reasoning and informed decision-making.

</details>


### [152] [MARC v1: An Open-Source Multi-Agent Framework for Clinical AI Reasoning and Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13476)
*Saisha Shetty,Satvik Tripathi,Austin Lin,Colin Zhao,Theodore Kim,Don Enwerem,Jacinta Arnold,Shahriar Faghani,Tessa S Cook*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出了一个名为MARC的开源多智能体推理与协调框架，用于临床推理任务，通过确定性多智能体编排替代单一LLM提示，并引入Decomposer模块自动生成提示，支持API和本地部署，无需代码修改即可配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单体LLM在临床推理中缺乏可解释性和失败定位能力，且手动提示工程繁琐，临床专家难以无编程使用。

Method: 设计角色专业化的智能体（提取、推理、答案生成、评估），通过显式上下文传递和可追踪的中间输出实现阶段级失败归因；引入Decomposer模块根据自然语言描述自动生成任务特定提示；模型无关，支持YAML配置及API/本地CPU部署。

Result: 提供了完整开源框架，支持解释性、可访问性和灵活部署，但未提供具体实验性能数据。

Conclusion: MARC旨在为临床领域专家提供无需编程的、可解释且模型无关的多智能体推理工具，促进临床推理的可追溯性和易用性。

Abstract: We present Multi-Agent Reasoning and Coordination (MARC), an open-source framework that replaces monolithic LLM prompting with deterministic multi-agent orchestration for clinical reasoning. MARC coordinates role-specialized agents for extraction, reasoning, answer generation, and evaluation, with explicit context passing and traceable intermediate outputs, enabling stage-wise failure attribution. We additionally introduce a Decomposer module that generates task-specific agent prompts from a plain-language description, eliminating manual prompt engineering. The framework supports both API-based and local CPU-compatible deployments and is entirely configurable via YAML, without code modifications. MARC is designed to be model-agnostic, interpretable, and accessible to clinical domain experts without programming expertise. The full framework is available at https://github.com/Penn-RAIL/MARC-v1.

</details>


### [153] [AlayaWorld: Interactive Long-Horizon World Modeling - Full Technical Report (v1.1)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13492)
*AlayaWorld Team,Kaipeng Zhang,Chuanhao Li,Yifan Zhan,Yongtao Ge,Yuanyang Yin,Jiaming Tan,Kang He,Liaoyuan Fan,Mingliang Zhai,Ruicong Liu,Xiaojie Xu,Xuangeng Chu,Zhen Li,Zhengyuan Lin,Zhixiang Wang,Zian Meng,Zihui Gao*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文介绍了AlayaWorld的改进版本，核心原则是让条件信号在潜在表示和时间结构上与生成内容尽可能匹配，通过六项具体修改实现了这一目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前版本中条件信号的表示和集成方式与生成内容在潜在空间和时间结构上不够一致，限制了生成质量与可控性。

Method: 采用流式3D点缓存渲染器替代深度扭曲空间记忆，并将视觉条件编码到与生成视频一致的因果VAE潜在空间。具体包括六项修改：运动感知潜在条件、空间记忆的因果编码、像素级时间记忆窗口对齐、硬记忆丢弃、统一VAE编解码协议、移除相机AdaLN分支。

Result: 新版本在条件信号与生成内容的一致性上显著提升，但在给定摘要中未提供定量结果。

Conclusion: 改进版AlayaWorld通过更紧密的条件-内容对齐，实现了更优的生成控制与质量，但需进一步实验验证其实际效果。

Abstract: This report presents an improved version of AlayaWorld. While the backbone architecture, chunk-wise autoregressive generation scheme, and training data remain unchanged from the previous release, we substantially revise how conditioning signals are represented and integrated into the model. The new design is guided by a simple principle: conditioning signals should match the generated content as closely as possible in both latent representation and temporal structure. To this end, we make two major changes. First, we replace the previous depth-warping-based spatial memory with a streaming 3D point-cache renderer. Second, we redesign the conditioning pipeline so that visual conditions are encoded in the same causal-VAE latent space, with temporal statistics consistent with those of the generated video. Concretely, the new version introduces six modifications: (1) replacing static-frame image conditioning with motion-aware latent conditioning; (2) causally encoding re-rendered spatial memory as a continuous sequence; (3) aligning the temporal-memory window in pixel space; (4) adopting hard memory dropout that removes memory tokens rather than zeroing them; (5) unifying the VAE encoding and decoding protocol across training and inference; and (6) removing the camera AdaLN branch, such that viewpoint control is provided entirely through the re-rendered spatial condition.

</details>


### [154] [QuoteBench: How Matched Scores Can Hide Command-Path Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13547)
*Shangao Li,Yao Zhang,Volker Tresp,Yuanyuan Yang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: LLM编码代理通过序列化、包装和重新解析模型输出的接口发出Bash命令，匹配执行分数无法区分命令生成错误与执行引入的失败。QuoteBench通过56个单次任务和14个事件衍生族，用额外解析器揭示生成契约与执行传输的边界，显示边界适应而非原始生成能力是模型间的主要差异，并建议评估应报告详细配置而非仅匹配分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估常使用匹配执行分数，但该分数掩盖了命令生成阶段与执行阶段之间的错误来源，无法真实反映模型能力，尤其在不同接口或解析器条件下，分数可能误导模型比较。

Method: 设计QuoteBench基准，包含56个从14个事件衍生族构建的单次任务，通过故意添加一个未转义的解析器来分离生成契约与执行传输；在8种相同窗口配置下，重放相同回复，比较原始路径与添加解析器后的成功率的差异，并测试披露边界后的恢复情况。

Result: 添加解析器使成功率降低55.4至73.2个百分点；披露边界后，6种配置恢复30.4至60.7个百分点，2种配置恢复为零或略负。原始生成接近饱和，但边界适应差异显著；例如GPT-5.6-sol的匹配差距-3.6分隐藏了-64.3分的损害和+60.7分的补偿。部署配置可重新排序模型，26对中有1对明确反转，4对处于单任务边缘。

Conclusion: 评估命令发出代理时，应报告模型配置、生成契约、执行路径、操作点和最终状态验证器，而不应将匹配分数视为模型内在属性，因为边界适应能力才是关键区分因素。

Abstract: LLM coding agents issue Bash commands through interfaces that may serialize, wrap, and reparse model output. Matched execution scores alone cannot distinguish command-generation errors from failures introduced after generation. QuoteBench measures this boundary with exact final-state validation on 56 one-shot tasks from 14 incident-derived families, crossing the generation contract with the execution transport around one deliberately unescaped added parser. Escaping at the interpolation point reproduces each replayed reply's raw-path outcome, so any recovery under a disclosed boundary must come from the model changing its generation. Across eight same-window configurations, replaying the same reply through the added parser lowers success by 55.4 to 73.2 percentage points; disclosure recovers 30.4 to 60.7 points for six configurations, and zero or slightly negative for the other two. Raw generation is nearly saturated at the frontier; boundary adaptation is what still separates models. GPT-5.6-sol's matched gap of -3.6 points hides -64.3 points of damage and +60.7 points of compensation. The deployment configuration reorders models: one reversal among 26 comparable pairs is unambiguous and four more sit on single-task margins. Evaluations of command-issuing agents should report the model configuration, generation contract, execution path, operating point, and final-state validator rather than treat a matched score as an intrinsic model property.

</details>


### [155] [OmniScientist: An Omni-Modal Omni-Discipline AI Scientist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13558)
*Bobo Li,Hao Fei,Tianjie Ju,Mong-Li Lee,Wynne Hsu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出OmniScientist，一个端到端全模态AI科学家系统，直接从异构原始证据执行多学科研究，在36个真实数据案例中完成从原始数据到成稿的全流程，相比仅用预计算标量特征的盲基线，在7个评估维度全面领先并赢得85%对比判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI科学家系统虽覆盖研究流程，但仅能处理文本、代码、标签或预计算摘要，无法获取科学发现所需的时空、跨通道及过程性证据，限制了证据 grounded 的发现能力。

Method: 系统由感知层和3个自主智能体（构思、实验、撰写）组成，采用确定性流水线，使观察贯穿研究全程；并通过代码执行想法、严谨性和声明检查，实现新颖性筛选、统计有效性、执行溯源和数值可追溯。

Result: 在跨越5个学科族、4类科学证据及图像、信号、音频、视频、3D结构、轨迹、表格、公式和图等多模态的36个案例中，全部完成从原始数据到可编译手稿的流程，参考推理骨干的平均论文分数为6.3；与仅接收预计算标量特征的盲变体相比，直接感知在全部7个评估维度上更优并赢得85%的成对比较。

Conclusion: 全生命周期感知对于基于证据的科学发现至关重要，该工作为构建广泛能力的AI科学家提供了切实可行的路径。

Abstract: Recent advances in foundation models have enabled AI scientists to automate increasingly complete research workflows, from hypothesis generation and code execution to manuscript preparation. Yet workflow coverage alone does not provide access to the full evidence on which scientific discovery depends. Existing systems typically reason over text, code, labels, or precomputed summaries, leaving scientifically decisive spatial, temporal, cross-channel, and procedural relations unavailable to the agent. We introduce OmniScientist, an end-to-end, omni-modal AI scientist that conducts multidisciplinary research directly from heterogeneous raw evidence. A perception layer and 3 autonomous agents for ideation, experiment, and writeup operate within a deterministic pipeline, allowing observations to shape research questions, experimental decisions, and final claims throughout the research lifecycle. By running idea, rigour, and claim checks in code, the system enforces novelty screening, statistical validity, execution provenance, and numerical traceability. We evaluate OmniScientist on 36 real-data cases spanning 5 discipline families, 4 families of scientific evidence, and modalities including images, signals, audio, video, 3-D structures, trajectories, tables, formulae, and graphs. The system completes the full path from raw data to a compiled manuscript in all 36 cases and achieves a mean overall paper score of 6.3 with the reference reasoning backbone. In paired comparisons against a blind variant that receives only precomputed scalar features, direct perception improves all 7 evaluation dimensions and wins 85% of head-to-head judgments. These results show that lifecycle-wide perception is essential for evidence-grounded scientific discovery and provides a practical path toward broadly capable AI scientists.

</details>


### [156] [Intern-S2-Preview: Scientific Agentic Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13505)
*Lei Bai,Jiaqi Cao,Chiyu Chen,Guanzhou Chen,Kai Chen,Guangran Cheng,Erfei Cui,Xuanlang Dai,Shengyuan Ding,Shangheng Du,Yanhui Duan,Yue Fan,Youqing Fang,Quan Gan,Yuanyuan Gao,Jiaye Ge,Lixin Gu,Yuzhe Gu,Qipeng Guo,Junjun He,Xin Hong,Ming Hu,Zhouqi Hua,Haian Huang,Junhao Huang,Zixian Huang,Minxi Jin,Lingkai Kong,Alexander Lam,Zehao Li,Zonglin Li,Tianhao Liang,Dahua Lin,Junyao Lin,Tianyang Lin,Zhouhan Lin,Jiangning Liu,Jin Liu,Kuikun Liu,Wenran Liu,Yifei Liu,Yuhong Liu,Yuhong Liu,Zhoumianze Liu,Ziyan Liu,Ziyu Liu,Haijun Lv,Han Lv,Chengqi Lyu,Le Ma,Ningsheng Ma,Zerun Ma,Haoyang Peng,Runyu Peng,Jifei Shan,Zixin Shang,Kou Shi,Xiang Shi,Qisheng Su,Xuerui Su,Hao Sun,Xiao Sun,Yanan Sun,Yu Sun,Huanze Tang,Yinghao Tang,Wenhui Tian,Zhongbo Tian,Bingli Wang,Haomin Wang,Jiarui Wang,Jingzhi Wang,Rui Wang,Xiquan Wang,Yi Wang,Zhecan Wang,Ziyi Wang,Zun Wang,Rubin Wei,Lianyi Wu,Wen Wu,Yue Wu,Yuhan Wu,Zhenyu Wu,Zijian Wu,Shuhao Xing,Jun Xu,Xingle Xu,Xuenan Xu,Xiangchao Yan,Ziang Yan,Bowen Yang,Danni Yang,Lin Yang,Zhiqi Yang,Qian Yao,Haochen Ye,Peng Ye,Jinhui Yin,Jiashuo Yu,Dingbo Yuan,Fei Yuan,Yuhang Zang,Bo Zhang,Chao Zhang,Chen Zhang,Hongjie Zhang,Junming Zhang,Wenlong Zhang,Wenwei Zhang,Yiming Zhang,Zhuo Zhang,Ziyang Zhang,Haiteng Zhao,Penghao Zhao,Yibo Zhao,Zhonghan Zhao,Zhihang Zhong,Bowen Zhou,Peiheng Zhou,Xin Zhou,Xinyu Zhou,Yunhua Zhou,Dongsheng Zhu,Yicheng Zou*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出了Intern-S2-Preview系列科学智能体基础模型，通过多模态预训练与统一后训练流程，支持科学理解、推理、生成及长程任务，在多项基准上取得领先结果，并验证了时间序列模块与记忆增强扩展的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现日益需要能跨异质模态推理、与科学工具环境交互并在长任务中持续进步的AI系统，现有模型在此类场景下能力不足。

Method: 采用科学多模态预训练（渲染文档、图文交错、科学语料），再经统一后训练管道：监督微调、多任务RL、黑/白盒智能体RL及在线蒸馏，配合部分rollout与离策略校正、自适应长度正则、在线投机解码、稳健多任务优化及轨迹感知经验组装等技巧。架构上，397B模型扩展时序建模至数值预测，并探索Memory Decoder作为独立记忆增强路径。

Result: Intern-S2-Preview-397B在科学、多模态、智能体及通用基准上表现竞争或领先；时序模块提升SciTS信号理解与预测；独立的Intern-MemDec-4B将Biology-Instructions平均分从56.92提升至60.32，且不改动冻结的397B主干。

Conclusion: 所提系列模型有效结合多模态预训练与复杂后训练策略，并证明时序建模与记忆增强扩展能进一步提升科学任务性能，为科学智能体基础模型提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Scientific discovery increasingly requires AI systems that can reason over scientific evidence of heterogeneous modalities, interact with scientific tools and environments, and sustain progress across long task horizons. We present Intern-S2-Preview, a series of scientific agentic foundation models designed to support multimodal scientific understanding, reasoning, generation, and long-horizon tasks. The training pipeline begins with scientific multimodal pre-training over rendered scientific documents, interleaved image-text data, and diverse scientific corpora. Starting from the pretrained checkpoint, we apply a unified post-training pipeline consisting of supervised fine-tuning, scalable multi-task reinforcement learning (RL), black- and white-box agentic RL, and on-policy distillation. This pipeline is supported by practical techniques that improve rollout and training stability and efficiency, including partial rollout with off-policy correction, adaptive length regularization, online speculative decoding, robust multi-task optimization, and trace-aware experience assembly for agentic tasks. At the architecture level, Intern-S2-Preview-397B extends time series modelling from efficient long-sequence understanding to numerical forecasting, while Memory Decoder is studied as a separate memory-augmented path for rapid scientific specialization without modifying the frozen 397B backbone. Evaluations across scientific, multimodal, agentic, and general-purpose benchmarks show that Intern-S2-Preview-397B achieves competitive or leading results in multiple settings. The time series modules improve scientific signal understanding and forecasting on SciTS, while the separate Intern-MemDec-4B extension improves the Biology-Instructions average score from 56.92 to 60.32 without modifying the frozen 397B backbone.

</details>


### [157] [Intervention-Aware Clinical World Model for Post-Op Outcome Forecasting in Cardiology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13518)
*Yunsung Chung,Yingshuo Liu,Abboud F. Hassan,Han Feng,Mary M. Maleckar,Nassir Marrouche,Jihun Hamm*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一种干预感知的临床世界模型，通过结构化潜在状态和异步事件序列建模术后恢复轨迹，在房颤消融复发预测中达到AUROC 0.756、AUPRC 0.777，并无需随访MRI即可预测瘢痕范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测模型将术后结果视为基线到终点的单步映射，忽略了恢复过程中异步记录的临床事件（如用药变化、重复干预）对风险的动态影响。

Method: 模型先编码基线影像为3D空间潜在状态，再通过手术背景、静态协变量、时间间隔和围术期生理嵌入逐步更新该状态；随访影像仅作为训练时潜在预测目标的监督信号。

Result: 在DECAAF-II重复内部交叉验证中，复发预测AUROC 0.756、AUPRC 0.777；瘢痕范围MAE为2.971个百分点，且推理时无需随访MRI。

Conclusion: 该模型能利用90天恢复期内的不规则术后记录提升长期复发风险预测，并支持不同时间窗口的风险查询和回顾性事件编辑，展示了结构化动态建模在临床预测中的价值。

Abstract: Many clinical prediction models treat post-intervention outcomes as a one-step mapping from baseline measurements to a future endpoint. However, recovery after a procedure often unfolds as an irregular trajectory: clinical observations, medication changes, repeat interventions, and physiological measurements are recorded asynchronously and can change risk assessment over time. We propose an intervention-aware clinical world model that represents each patient with a structured latent state and evolves it through time-ordered post-intervention events. The model first encodes baseline imaging into a 3D spatial latent state. It then updates this state using procedural context, static covariates, elapsed time, and peri-event physiological embeddings. Follow-up imaging provides training-only supervision through a latent forecasting objective. We apply the framework to atrial fibrillation ablation. During the 90-day recovery window, irregular post-procedure records provide clinically meaningful evidence for long-term recurrence risk. In repeated internal cross-validation on DECAAF-II, our model achieves AUROC 0.756 and AUPRC 0.777 for recurrence prediction. It also achieves a scar-extent MAE of 2.971 percentage points without requiring follow-up MRI intensities at inference. The learned state supports recurrence-risk queries at different horizons and retrospective input editing of blanking-period records.

</details>


### [158] [DARTree: Speculative Diffusion Decoding with Autoregressive Draft Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13524)
*Tianyi Li,Yaxin Luo,Xinyi Shang,Zhiqiang Shen*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: DARTree是一种无需训练的投机解码方法，通过将预训练的自回归修正头从链扩展到树，在保持无损加速的同时显著提升接受长度与解码速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有投机解码方法中，扩散式草稿模型虽能并行预测整个token块，但其分布是边缘化的，未沿草稿路径条件化；递归修正仅沿单链引入因果信息，而扩散式树构建虽扩大候选覆盖却未在分支间传递修正。两者优势未能结合。

Method: DARTree构建固定宽度的候选树，在每层深度批量扩展并评分所有节点，随后仅用最佳优先剪枝选择验证树，从而将自回归修正头推理与顺序堆操作解耦。

Result: 在七个数学、代码与聊天基准上，DARTree在所有四种模型-温度配置中取得最高平均接受长度与加速比，单轮验证最多接受12.97个token，比DFlash高98.6%，比Domino高27.9%，相对本地自回归解码可达9.73倍无损加速。

Conclusion: DARTree通过树结构并行化与高效剪枝策略，有效融合扩散式草稿的并行性与自回归修正的因果性，实现了更优的投机解码性能。

Abstract: Speculative decoding losslessly accelerates autoregressive language models by verifying multiple draft tokens in parallel. Diffusion-based drafters further reduce proposal latency by predicting an entire token block in parallel, but their position-wise distributions are marginal rather than conditioned on tokens selected along each draft path. Existing recurrent correction incorporates causal information along a single draft chain, whereas diffusion-based tree construction broadens candidate coverage without carrying this correction along individual branches. We introduce DARTree, a training-free speculative decoding method that extends a pretrained AR correction head from chains to trees. DARTree first constructs a fixed-width candidate tree by expanding and scoring all nodes at each depth in a single batch, and then only applies best-first pruning to select the verification tree, decoupling AR-head inference from sequential heap operations. Across seven math, code, and chat benchmarks, DARTree achieves the highest average acceptance length and speedup in all four model--temperature configurations, accepting up to 12.97 tokens per verification round, 98.6\% more than DFlash and 27.9\% more than Domino in the same setting, and reaching up to 9.73$\times$ lossless speedup over locally measured autoregressive decoding.

</details>


<div id='Model'></div>

# Model [[Back]](#toc)

### [159] [Which Site, and When: A Free-Satellite-Data Test of Himalayan Glacial Lake Bursts, Landslides, and Ice Floods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12422)
*Matthew Kahn,Milan Arjel,Nirmala Adhikari,Mingmar Sherpa,James Pope*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本研究利用免费卫星数据（雷达干涉测量和气象数据）预测尼泊尔喜马拉雅地区的冰川湖溃决、滑坡和小型洪水风险，发现气象数据能有效预测触发时机（ROC 0.73-0.83），而地形对易发性预测仅在部分情况下有效（ROC 0.54-0.76），简单模型优于深度学习，并提供排名监测清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可行性研究仅指出变形指示湖泊失稳、天气指示风险时段，但未提出预测模型。为填补这一空白，本研究旨在利用免费数据构建可验证的预测框架，区分易感地点和触发时机，并确保模型在空间交叉验证下不依赖训练邻域信息。

Method: 针对三种灾害（大型冰碛/冰坝溃决、降雨型滑坡、冰川小型洪水），分别回答两个独立问题（易感性、触发时机）。使用589起溃决事件和数千起滑坡记录，匹配相似未失败点作为对照。采用空间交叉验证（按地图瓦片保留）和强基线比较，测试地形、前期气象（如降水）和多种深度学习模型（对比梯度提升树），并提取简单决策树规则。

Result: 前期气象在触发时机预测上表现稳定（大溃决ROC 0.73，滑坡0.83，小洪水0.82）；地形易感性在朴素评估下接近0.9，但匹配相似地点后降至0.76、0.71和0.54（后者无预测价值）。尼泊尔单独区域的大溃决预测ROC达0.89。深度学习模型未显著超越梯度提升基线，后者在湖泊灾害中胜出，可简化为基于粗糙度和季风降雨的三条规则。

Conclusion: 免费数据足以支撑风险时机的预警（尤其气象触发），但地形易感性预测能力有限，特定区域（如尼泊尔）效果较好。简单模型和可解释规则更可靠，深度学习无显著优势。最终提供尼泊尔排名监测清单作为优先级参考而非精确预测，并指出免费数据在分辨率和覆盖上的局限性。

Abstract: Two free satellite signals carry real information about glacial-lake outburst risk in the Nepal Himalaya: radar interferometry sees a moraine dam slowly sagging, and satellite weather marks the weeks when a primed lake is under stress. A companion feasibility study found that deformation indicates which lake is destabilizing and weather indicates when it is at risk, but proposed no predictive model. To address this gap, we propose and evaluate models that predict which site is susceptible and when a trigger arrives. We test three related hazards on free data alone: large moraine- and ice-dammed bursts, rainfall-triggered landslides, and smaller floods from ponds on and around a glacier. Each hazard gets two questions, never blended. Using 589 dated outbursts from HMAGLOFDB and several thousand catalogued landslides, we match each event against similar but unfailed sites, and hold every model to a strong simple baseline under spatial cross-validation that withholds whole map tiles, so no model succeeds by recognising a trained-on neighbourhood. Antecedent weather times the trigger at ROC 0.73 for big bursts, 0.83 for landslides, and 0.82 for small floods. Terrain ranks susceptibility only in part: scored naively it appears near 0.9, largely because catalogued failures cluster in wetter ranges; matched against comparable nearby sites the honest figures are 0.76, 0.71, and 0.54 (no better than chance). The burst signal holds within single regions, reaching 0.89 in Nepal alone. Five deep-learning models do not decisively beat a simple gradient-boosted baseline. Three score marginally higher on landslides, a hint too small to confirm. For the lake hazards the baseline wins outright, reproduced by a three-rule decision tree on ruggedness and monsoon rainfall. We close with a ranked Nepal watchlist, a prioritisation aid, not a prediction, and note where free data reaches its limits.

</details>


### [160] [Geometric and Behavioral Stratification in Transformer Residual Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12447)
*Nelson Guda*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 在Transformer模型中，预测方向（unembedding方向）作为内容定义的优先锚点，将残差流按与预测的接近程度进行几何和行为分层；该分层在所有18个模型中稳定存在，且预测近端区域结构高度有序、因果关键，远端区域平坦但承载计算负载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究识别了Transformer模型中的优先基（坐标轴统计异常），但对这些基选择的具体方向及几何结构缺乏理解；方差类分析因预测方向与主方差轴近正交而无法完整揭示组织，需寻找新的锚定视角。

Method: 以模型当前预测token的unembedding方向为锚点，测量残差流向量在该方向上的投影距离，分析其几何统计（如方差聚类）、行为关联（提示分组区分度）及因果扰动（沿不同层级方向加噪观察输出分歧时间）。

Result: 预测方向近端残差变化集中且高度结构化，能聚类相关提示；远端平坦且对提示组反区分。扰动预测近端方向立即引起发散和任务框架切换，扰动次级方向延迟发散但保持框架，远端方向弱对齐但因果和时间上承载。尺度不变且随模型规模增大远端扩展。

Conclusion: 预测方向是区别于已描述坐标轴的独特优先锚点，揭示了高维计算与线性读出共存的几何机制：窄接口承载决定性的读出结构，而宽远端补充提供计算支撑，行为由方向而非幅值主导。

Abstract: Trained transformer models develop privileged bases: coordinate axes whose statistics differ from the rest of the residual stream. But what kind of direction does such a basis select? We investigate the prediction direction, the unembedding direction of the token a model currently predicts, and find that it functions as a content-defined privileged anchor. Measured with respect to this anchor, residual-stream variation is geometrically and behaviorally stratified by proximity to the prediction.
  The stratification holds in all eighteen models tested (dense and mixture-of-experts, 7B-120B, base and instruction-tuned). A narrow, scale-invariant prediction interface concentrates readout-relevant structure, while the vast prediction-distal complement expands with model scale. Because the prediction direction sits nearly orthogonal to the principal variance axes, variance-based analyses recover this organization only partly, and the shortfall grows with prompt heterogeneity.
  Anchoring reveals a steep geometric gradient: prediction-proximal regions are highly structured and cluster related prompts, while the complement is flatter and anti-discriminates among prompt groups. The interface is a narrow slice but functionally decisive. Disrupting the variance directions closest to the prediction causes immediate divergence and frequent task-frame shifts; disrupting the next level down delays divergence and preserves framing. The complement is weakly readout-aligned per direction yet causally and temporally load-bearing, and behavior is driven by direction rather than magnitude.
  These results establish the prediction direction as a privileged anchor distinct from previously described coordinate axes, and give a geometric account of how high-dimensional computation coexists with linear readout.

</details>


### [161] [Exemplar-based objective classification of gust-induced loads across multiple flight conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12448)
*Paolo Olivucci,Kowshik Srivatsan,David E. Rival*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出一种基于机器学习表示与摘要选取的客观分类准则，将3480个阵风载荷测量数据归纳为9种跨飞行姿态重复出现的基本响应类型，且保持可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于粗参数（如飞行姿态）的标签分类难以组织复杂阵风载荷数据，且缺乏客观性；需要一种既客观又能保持物理可解释性的分类方法。

Method: 先用机器学习编码大量实验观测，再通过摘要程序选出最小高代表性样本集，这些样本作为相似性判据对所有观测进行分类。

Result: 在飞翼模型六个飞行姿态、3480组随机阵风压力载荷数据中发现9种基本响应类型，跨姿态重复出现；瞬态响应分析揭示了底层流体力学物理机制。

Conclusion: 该方法能够提供客观、可解释且可指导精细实验的阵风载荷分类准则，有助于理解复杂气动现象。

Abstract: Is it possible to find an objective classification criterion that organizes the complexity of gust-induced loads across many flight conditions? And one that remains as interpretable as a labelling based on coarse parameters, such as the flight attitude? Our approach encodes a large number of experimental observations through a machine-learned representation and applies a summarization procedure to select a minimal subset of highly significant exemplars. The exemplars provide a similarity-based objective classification criterion of all the observations, they can be more conveniently inspected by experts and can become subject of more refined experiments. We demonstrate the approach on a database of 3480 pressure-load measurements induced by random gusts on a flying-wing model across six flight attitudes. We find nine fundamental response types that recur across multiple attitudes; analysis of a type's transient response enables physical intuition into the underlying fluid mechanics.

</details>


### [162] [Exploring Oversmoothing with Householder Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12514)
*Bhaskar Karol*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出HouseGNN，用Householder反射与GroupSort构建逐段正交层，缓解深度GNN的过平滑问题，并证明其范数保持、尺度/符号不变性及距离变化机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度GNN因归一化传播算子反复作用于隐表示，导致节点特征向低信息子空间塌缩，即过平滑，削弱深层表示能力。

Method: HouseGNN不直接更新隐状态，而是用聚合邻域消息估计反射方向，通过Householder反射器和GroupSort组成逐段正交层，每个节点每层保持欧氏范数。

Result: 证明了三个核心性质：每层节点范数保持、反射器对消息尺度与符号不变、节点间距离可通过不匹配的正交算子改变。

Conclusion: 该设计通过正交更新避免信息坍缩，为深层GNN提供理论保障，有望缓解过平滑并增强表达能力。

Abstract: Deep graph neural networks(GNNs) suffer from oversmoothing- a progressive collapse of node representation towards a low information subspace as network depth increases because the normalized graph propagation operator is repeatedly applied directly to the hidden representations. In this work we study Householder Graph Neural Network (HouseGNN). Rather than updating the hidden state like standard GCN, HouseGNN uses the aggregated neighbourhood message solely to estimate a reflection direction; the node embedding is then updated by a Householder reflector followed by GroupSort, yielding a piecewise orthogonal layer that preserves Euclidean norm at every node and at every depth. We prove three core properties: (i) every internal layer preserves the node-wise Euclidean norm; (ii) the Householder reflector is scale scale and sign-invariant in the message; and (iii) pairwise distance between nodes can change through mismatch between node-wise orthogonal operators.

</details>


### [163] [A Commitment-Based Hybrid Post-Quantum Cryptographic Model for Multi-File Cloud Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13138)
*Lemdi Frank Prikutse,Regina Esi Turkson,Alimatu-Saadia Yussiff,Abdul-Lateef Yussiff,Maame G. Asante-Mensah*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出基于批承诺的混合后量子模型，将批量文件上传的签名次数从n次降至1次，显著降低认证开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云存储客户端需要抵御未来量子攻击的认证，朴素混合方案按文件签名导致大规模批量上传时开销不可接受。

Method: 使用AES-256-GCM加密、X25519+ML-KEM-768密钥封装、Ed25519+ML-DSA-65双签名，并对批量密文计算SHA3-256承诺后仅签名一次。

Result: 签名阶段时间随批量大小保持近恒定，n=1000时相比逐文件双签名基线减少约606-725倍。

Conclusion: 该模型有效消除批量上传中的后量子签名瓶颈，保持安全性且性能随批量扩展。

Abstract: Cloud storage clients increasingly require authentication that remains secure against future quantum-capable adversaries, motivating hybrid constructions that combine classical primitives with standardized post-quantum alternatives. Extended naively to multi-file upload, such constructions incur a per-file lattice signing cost that dominates authentication time and becomes prohibitive at realistic batch sizes. This paper presents a commitment-based hybrid post-quantum model that addresses this bottleneck. It comprises AES-256-GCM bulk encryption, a hybrid X25519 with ML-KEM-768 key encapsulation mechanism, and a hybrid Ed25519 with ML-DSA-65 dual signature, computed over a SHA3-256 batch commitment. The commitment binds all ciphertexts in a batch to a single fixed-size digest that is signed once, reducing the number of post-quantum signature invocations per batch from n to one, independent of batch size; the remaining encryption and hashing is bounded by fast symmetric throughput. On a commodity client platform, averaged over 20 repetitions, this holds signing-phase time near-constant as the batch grows while the per-file baseline scales linearly. At n = 1000, the model reduces signing-phase time by factors of 629, 606, and 725 for 100 KB, 1 MB, and 10 MB files respectively, against a per-file dual-signing baseline sharing every other primitive.

</details>


### [164] [CAS: A Causal Attribution Score for Local and Global Explainable Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12555)
*Michael Georgiades,Charalambia Varnava*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出因果归因分数（CAS），将预测解释与干预效应区分，并通过局部到全局的因果报告层提供因果解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测解释方法仅归因模型输出，未归因干预对真实结果的影响，需要一种明确的因果归因框架。

Method: 基于识别出的干预合作博弈，用因果Shapley分解联合干预对比，生成局部CAS、符号局部CAS及两种全局CAS摘要，无需新Shapley公式。

Result: 在已知真值基准中，CAS平均MAE为0.107，优于单次归一化（0.173）和全局ATE向量（0.213）；在经验数据中，预测SHAP与特征级CAS排名差异显著，如Pennsylvania中dep1从预测第13升至因果第2。

Conclusion: CAS有效分离预测重要性与因果效应异质性，为干预目标提供更准确的解释。

Abstract: Predictive explanation methods attribute a model output; they do not, by themselves, attribute an intervention effect on the real-world outcome. We introduce the Causal Attribution Score (CAS), a compact score architecture for causal explanation. CAS starts from an identified interventional coalition game, allocates the joint intervention contrast with causal Shapley contributions, and converts those raw outcome-scale effects into Local CAS, Signed Local CAS, and two complementary Global CAS summaries. The innovation is not a new Shapley formula, but a local-to-global causal reporting layer with an explicit intervention target. In the known-truth benchmark, eight repeated primary-interaction simulations (n = 2,200 each, three actions) gave mean Local CAS MAE of 0.107 for coalition-aware CAS, compared with 0.173 for one-at-a-time normalisation and 0.213 for a global normalised absolute ATE vector. The paired advantage over one-at-a-time normalisation increased from -0.003 under additivity to 0.091 under strong interactions. On both empirical DoubleML datasets, 401(k) eligibility/net financial assets (n = 9,915) and Pennsylvania reemployment bonus/unemployment duration (n = 5,099), predictive SHAP/TreeSHAP rankings differed materially from Feature-CAS rankings of treatment-effect modifiers. In Pennsylvania, dep1 (exactly one dependent) moved from predictive global rank 13 to Feature-CAS rank 2 and was the leading local Feature-CAS modifier. These results isolate the added value of separating what predicts the outcome from what explains heterogeneity in an estimated causal effect.

</details>


### [165] [Represent, Then Generate: Multimodal-Conditioned Time-Series Generation under Irregular Missingness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12592)
*Haochen Zhang,Jiaheng Guo,Yu-Chao Huang,Nicholas Knoz,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出ReCoGen两阶段框架，先逐模态训练掩码自编码器提取耐缺失的紧凑令牌，再用流匹配生成器融合时间与静态条件合成目标信号，在16个设置中全部超越6个基线，13个达到或超过真实信号效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有条件生成器仅支持单一模态，难以处理临床中多模态信号混合、不规则缺失及静态变量并存的实际场景，需要一种能统一表征异构条件并生成缺失目标信号的方法。

Method: 阶段一用掩码自编码器将每个时间变化模态压缩为耐缺失的令牌序列；阶段二用流匹配生成器，通过可学习交叉注意力融合冻结的逐模态编码器输出，静态条件则走令牌加自适应层归一化双路径。

Result: 在AI-READI连续血糖、MIMIC-III/IV动脉血压三个基准上，16个（数据集、任务、指标）设置全部最优，其中13个达到或超过真实信号效用，消融证实增益来自交叉注意力与双路径静态条件。

Conclusion: ReCoGen能把常规采集信号转化为有信息量的替代信号，支持无创或低成本连续监测，且将真实信号效用视为近似参考而非上限，展示出超越真实数据潜力的可能。

Abstract: Continuous physiological time series underpin modern clinical monitoring, yet many of the most informative signals are invasive, expensive, or simply unavailable for a given patient. Conditional generation offers a remedy: an absent signal can be synthesized from co-recorded signals and routine clinical variables. Existing generators, however, are built around a single conditioning modality and degrade when forced to handle the heterogeneous, irregularly missing mix of time-variant signals and static covariates seen in practice. We propose ReCoGen (Represent Conditions, then Generate), a two-stage framework that decouples multimodal condition representation from target generation. Stage I trains one masked autoencoder per modality, distilling each time-variant condition into a compact and missingness-tolerant token sequence. Stage II trains a flow-matching generator that fuses these tokens with static conditions to synthesize the target signal. Across three physiological benchmarks, including continuous glucose monitoring on AI-READI and arterial blood pressure generation on MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV, ReCoGen attains the best downstream utility on all sixteen (dataset, task, metric) settings, surpassing six representative conditional generators; on thirteen of them its utility also reaches or exceeds the utility measured on the real signal, a reference we read as an approximate anchor rather than a ceiling. Ablations trace the gains to the conditioning path: learnable cross-attention over the frozen per-modality encoders, and a dual token-plus-AdaLN route for the static conditions. ReCoGen thus turns routinely collected signals into informative surrogates for invasive or unavailable ones, a step toward less invasive, lower-cost continuous clinical monitoring.

</details>


### [166] [The Boolean Power of ReLU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12617)
*Pablo Barceló,Floris Geerts,Matthias Lanzinger,Klara Pakhomenko,Jan Van den Bussche*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文证明了在带单一布尔节点特征的有限简单无向图上，对于任意由最终恒定激活函数组成的集合Σ，Σ-MPLang可表达的布尔查询严格少于ReLU-MPLang，从而解决了ReLU-MPLang是否比trReLU-MPLang在布尔查询上更强大的开放问题，并推出ReLU-GNN严格强于{TrReLU,id}-GNN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前有开放问题关注ReLU-MPLang与trReLU-MPLang在布尔查询表达能力上的差异，本文旨在严格证明ReLU-MPLang更具表达力，并澄清ReLU-GNN与TrReLU,id-GNN在布尔特征图上的表达能力层级。

Method: 通过构造特定的布尔查询或图论性质，利用最终恒定激活函数的逼近或分离性质，对比两类语言的可定义性，建立严格包含关系。

Result: 对于任意Σ（最终恒定激活函数集合），Σ-MPLang的布尔查询类严格包含于ReLU-MPLang的布尔查询类，即存在ReLU-MPLang可表达但任何Σ-MPLang不可表达的布尔查询。

Conclusion: ReLU-MPLang在布尔查询上严格强于所有最终恒定激活函数类，特别地，ReLU-GNN在布尔特征图上的表达能力严格超越{TrReLU,id}-GNN。

Abstract: We prove that, on finite simple undirected graphs equipped with a single Boolean node feature, the Boolean queries expressible in $Σ$-MPLang, for any collection $Σ$ of eventually constant activation functions and with arbitrary real coefficients, form a strict subclass of the Boolean queries expressible in ReLU-MPLang. We thereby settle a recently posed open problem: whether ReLU-MPLang is more powerful than trReLU-MPLang when it comes to Boolean queries. In particular, this implies that ReLU-GNNs are strictly more expressive than {TrReLU,id}-GNNs with respect to Boolean queries on Boolean-featured graphs.

</details>


### [167] [TeleGapper: On the (un)reliability of Privacy Policies in Telegram Mini apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13390)
*Luca Ferrari,Mariano Ceccato,Luca Verderame*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: TeleGapper是一个针对Telegram Mini Apps的黑盒动态分析框架，通过捕获运行时网络流量并与隐私披露对比，发现59.4%的应用联系未披露的第三方、78.8%仅依赖默认隐私政策、且无任何同意或退出机制，揭示该生态存在严重透明度问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Telegram Mini Apps虽广泛使用且可访问平台上下文，但其隐私属性未被充分研究；与微信等受控架构不同，其WebView模型结合标准Web功能和不受限外联网络，使敏感信息可能通过普通Web请求泄露，而现有隐私政策可能无法准确反映实际数据实践。

Method: 提出TeleGapper，一种黑盒动态分析框架，通过捕获运行时的网络流量、识别第三方通信，并将观察到的数据流与隐私披露信息进行比较，以评估Mini Apps的隐私态势；评估对象为从tApps Center收集的278个可用Mini Apps。

Result: 研究发现59.4%的应用至少联系一个未披露的第三方，78.8%完全依赖Telegram的默认隐私政策，且没有任何应用提供同意或退出机制。

Conclusion: Telegram Mini Apps生态存在显著的透明度和合规性差距，在广泛使用但研究不足的环境中，用户隐私缺乏有效保障，亟需改进隐私披露与实践一致性。

Abstract: Telegram Mini Apps are Web applications embedded within the Telegram client, forming an ecosystem of third-party services within one of the world's most widely used messaging platforms. Despite their growing adoption and access to Telegram-provided context, their privacy properties remain largely unexplored. Unlike ecosystems such as WeChat, which rely on tightly controlled, proprietary execution frameworks, Telegram adopts a different model: Mini Apps run inside a WebView, combining platform-provided context with standard Web capabilities and unrestricted outbound networking. This enables applications to transmit sensitive information to analytics, advertising, tracking, or other third parties through ordinary Web requests, often with limited visibility.
  Privacy disclosures are therefore critical for transparency. Telegram allows Mini Apps either to define an application-specific privacy policy or to rely on a platform-provided default policy. While the latter reduces the developer's disclosure burden, it may lead to generic statements that do not accurately capture actual data practices of individual Mini Apps.
  In this paper, we present TeleGapper, a black-box dynamic analysis framework to assess the privacy posture of Mini Apps by capturing runtime network traffic, identifying third-party communications, and comparing observed data flows against disclosed privacy information. We evaluate 278 working Mini Apps collected from tApps Center, a community-driven catalogue for discovering third-party applications in Telegram. We find that 59.4% contact at least one undisclosed third party, 78.8% rely exclusively on Telegram's default privacy policy, and none provides a consent or opt-out mechanism. These findings expose a substantial transparency and compliance gap in a widely used yet understudied ecosystem.

</details>


### [168] [DiG-bench: Discovery in Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12593)
*Ruairidh M. Battleday,Kai Sandbrink,Jimi Cullen-Drohan,Zihan Yan,Timothy Muller,Clare Maguire,Ales Kubicek,Fraser Greenlee-Scott,Sukrit Sumant,Tri Dao,Jürgen Schmidhuber,Michal Valko,Joshua Tenenbaum,Thomas L. Griffiths,Zeb Kurth-Nelson,James C. R. Whittington*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: DiG-bench是一个通过70个独立游戏测试AI科学发现能力（在未知规则和未知目标条件下通过交互实验发现新知识）的新基准，分7个难度层级，其中21个公开、49个私有。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI基准缺少对‘发现新知识’这一科学过程核心能力的直接测试，尤其是在受控环境中目标未知时通过实验进行探索的能力。

Method: 设计70个由短字符串编码、具有独特变换规则的游戏，玩家需在未知规则和关卡获胜条件的条件下通过交互实验发现规则，并按难度分为7级以覆盖从常规到顶尖智能体的能力范围。

Result: 最低难度可被多种模型解决，最高难度挑战最强代理模型；所有70个游戏均有至少一位人类首次尝试即成功解决，但未提供AI的具体解决比例。

Conclusion: DiG-bench填补了发现类任务基准的空白，为评估AI在未知目标下的科学发现能力提供了受控且分难度的测试环境，并预留私有集保障评估安全性。

Abstract: Discovery---formulating novel generalizations---is a central part of the scientific process. Despite its importance, there is a gap in the current AI benchmark landscape, with few benchmarks directly probing the capacity for discovering new knowledge with experimentation in controlled environments where the objective is unknown. To address this gap, we release a new benchmark: DiG-bench (Discovery in Games). DiG-bench consists of a set of 70 independent games. Each game is encoded as a short string and has unique transformation rules that must be discovered through interaction and experimentation. The levels of the game present a series of challenges to test whether the rules have been discovered, where the win conditions for each level are also unknown. We provide games at seven tiers of difficulty for AI agents. The lowest tier is routinely solvable by multiple models, while the highest tier challenges the best models in agentic harnesses. All 70 games were solved by at least one human on first attempt. A subset of 21 games is released publicly, and the remainder is held private for secure evaluation.

</details>


### [169] [Dead text or binding clause? Measuring and restoring constraint influence in black-box LLM dialogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12599)
*Haoyuan Zhu*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出Sysname系统，通过合约账本、消融探针和修复阶梯，在API层面实现对话中约束撤销的测量、预测与修复，显著降低行为复发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮对话中撤销约束常被模型忽略，导致行为复发，且缺乏测量、预测和修复手段。

Method: 合约账本记录约束及执行器、撤销墓碑并预编译净状态；顺序消融探针测量逐条约束影响；修复阶梯在匹配预算下修复。

Result: 复发随约束增加而上升，编译显著减少复发（p值显著），探针预测AUROC高，墓碑注释恢复部分效果，开销小。

Conclusion: 撤销失败变为可测、可预测、可修复的对话状态属性，而非隐性问题。

Abstract: Multi-turn dialogues let users revoke constraints as easily as impose them, but revocation does not reliably take effect: models keep enacting withdrawn requirements (occasionally beneath comments asserting their removal), a failure we call \emph{behavioral relapse}, or revocation inertia. No existing instrument measures this influence per clause, predicts it before delivery, or repairs it under matched budgets. \sysname{} closes the three gaps through the model API alone: a contract ledger pairs every constraint with an executable checker, records revocations as tombstones, and compiles the net constraint state ahead of time into a single specification; a sequential ablation probe measures per-clause adherence and incremental behavioral effect; a repair ladder operates under token- and attempt-matched budgets. On \dataname{} (\NTasks{} HumanEval tasks, \NClauses{} verified checkers), relapse at an 8B operating point climbs from \ScaleDelayedMTwo{} to \ScaleDelayedMEight{} as constraint load grows, while stronger models sit at floor. Under matched checkers, model, and budget, ahead-of-time compilation significantly reduces relapse against a no-ledger verifier-retry baseline (\RestoreDiff{}, 95\% CI \RestoreDiffCI{}, $p$ \RestoreDiffP{}); adaptive ladder interventions stacked on top add no detectable gain (95\% confidence excludes gains $\geq$ \LadderExcludedGain{}). The probe predicts relapse before delivery (AUROC \AurocPrimary{}); a one-sentence tombstone note recovers about a third of the compilation effect and survives a placebo control. At \CostDeliveryFactor{} delivery overhead and \CostTotalHedged{} of API compute for every result, revocation failure becomes a measurable, predictable, and repairable property of dialogue state rather than an invisible one.

</details>


### [170] [@skills: Attention is all you have](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12610)
*Li Yin,Zhi Li,Zhan Shi,Haoran Zhang,Haebin Seong,Zhangyang,Wang*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出 @skills 开放协议，将技能安装拆分为内容、持久化和自动触发三部分，仅触发需驻留提示词；通过路径引用技能，实现“无需安装、读取即用”，并结合可选中心化 Hub 提供搜索与托管。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公共技能数量庞大但安装模型受限，系统提示词仅有不足100个可靠触发槽，导致长尾技能无法使用，团队内部技能也难以竞争空间；安装机制将内容、持久化与自动触发不必要地捆绑，造成资源浪费。

Method: @skills 协议：路径寻址任意技能/子树/集合；读取即可用，不安装不驻留；vendoring 操作复制到项目Git树；添加一行 .gitignore 式条目实现触发；目录即菜单；无 manifest、锁文件或注册；SKILL.md 不变；通过单指令文件使任意能读文件、执行命令的代理成为客户端。

Result: 协议已开源（GitHub链接），并在AdaL CLI中实现；配套免费Hub提供搜索、排名、免仓库托管、私有/团队集合及单屏编写功能；本地和gh:路径无需Hub，索引的GitHub技能保留原身份。

Conclusion: 通过解耦安装功能，实现“少安装，多使用”，显著提升长尾技能可达性，同时保持协议兼容性、开放性和增量部署便利性。

Abstract: There are 56,804 public agent skills today, and teams write many more privately. The dominant delivery model is installation: once installed, a skill's description remains in the system prompt, competing for fewer than 100 reliable trigger slots. This leaves the long tail with no practical path to use and forces teams' own playbooks to compete for the same scarce space. We observe that installation bundles three separable functions: content, persistence, and automatic triggering. Only the last requires prompt residency. We therefore propose @skills, an open protocol that separates them. A path addresses any skill, subtree, or collection, and reading a skill is sufficient to use it, so nothing is installed or made resident. The operation vendors a copy at the same path into a project's Git-tracked tree for adaptation and ownership. The operation adds one .gitignore-style line, the only element that costs prompt residency. A directory is a menu, making bundles ordinary directories rather than all-or-nothing units. The protocol requires no manifest, lockfile, or registration, and SKILL.md remains unchanged. @skills is additive, ships as an installable package, and turns any agent that can read files and run commands into a client through a single instruction file. Its open specification is at https://github.com/SylphAI-Inc/atskills and it is implemented in the AdaL CLI at https://adalagent.ai . Because paths address skills well but cannot find them, the protocol is paired with a free hub at https://atskills.one for corpus-wide search and ranking, repository-free hosting, private and team collections, and one-screen authoring. The hub is optional: gh: and local paths resolve without it, and indexed GitHub skills retain their gh: identities. Install less, use more.

</details>


### [171] [Demand Transfer Estimation at Scale via Restricted Logit Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12680)
*Lakshya Garg,Deep Narayan Mishra,Swapnil Yadav,Haoan Wang,Sujal Alugubelli,Karthik Kumaran,Anupriya Sharma*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出一种在大规模商品集合（100万+商品）上高效估计需求转移（DT）系数的方法，用于在独立需求预测基础上修正商品间的替代影响，提升需求预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于选择模型的需求预测需对每个可能的商品组合单独计算期望需求，在大规模商品类别中效率极低；而独立预测虽高效但忽略商品间替代关系，导致预测不准。因此需一种能结合两者优点的方法。

Method: 核心是估计DT系数，表示当目标商品（顾客原本想买的商品）缺货时，其需求转移到其他商品的比例。作者设计了适用于超大规模商品集合的DT系数计算流程，并利用交易历史数据及替代行为假设进行估计。

Result: 在多个类别的多门店真实交易数据上验证，当满足合理替代行为假设时，该方法能准确估计底层DT系数，并显著改进需求预测效果。

Conclusion: 所提方法为大规模商品组合优化中的需求预测提供了高效且准确的解决方案，兼具独立预测的可扩展性和替代效应修正的准确性。

Abstract: Item demand forecasting is an integral component of store assortment optimization. Existing literature focuses on learning a suitable customer choice model and using this model to determine the value of an objective function (i.e. expected demand) with respect to an assortment proposal. However, for large item universe with many categories, this approach can prove inefficient, needing a separate demand forecast for every possible item assortment. An alternate approach exists whereby we combine the efficiency of forecasting item demand independently, while at the same time applying adjustments to the independent forecasts that account for the relations between item demand and the availability of other similar items on the shelf.
  Central to this approach is the estimation of Demand Transfer (DT) coefficients. These DT coefficients represent the percent of a particular target item's (item that the customer walked in the store to buy) demand that is redirected to each other item in the universe should the target item be removed from the shelf. We introduce an approach that allows us to compute these DT coefficients on large item universes (assortments having 1 million+ items). Experiments on data as well as historical transaction data for multiple locations within categories demonstrate that when certain reasonable assumptions about substitution behavior are satisfied, our procedure is able to accurately estimate underlying DT coefficients and lead to improvements in demand forecasting.

</details>


### [172] [General Probabilities of Causation with Causal Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12657)
*Xin Shu,Zhen Lei,Ang Li*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文通过纳入协变量和中介变量中的因果信息，推导了多值因果概率的更紧边界，并通过示例和模拟验证其优于现有非二元边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已为二元及多值因果概率（如PN、PS、PNS）推导了理论边界，但多值情形下能否利用额外因果知识进一步收紧边界仍属开放问题。

Method: 将协变量与中介变量蕴含的因果信息整合进多值PoCs的偏识别框架，基于结构方程模型推导出更紧的理论边界，并辅以玩具示例与模拟研究验证。

Result: 所提边界在理论上严格更紧，模拟研究表明其窄于现有非二元边界，展示了额外因果信息的收紧效用。

Conclusion: 多值因果概率边界可借助协变量和中介信息显著收紧，为不可观测个体因果响应的推断提供了更精确的工具。

Abstract: Probabilities of causation (PoCs) characterize individual causal responses that cannot be directly observed and therefore generally require partial identification. Tian and Pearl first derived theoretically sharp bounds for binary PoCs, including the probability of necessity (PN), the probability of sufficiency (PS), and the probability of necessity and sufficiency (PNS). Mueller et al. subsequently tightened the bounds for binary PNS by incorporating causal information encoded in covariates and mediators. More recently, Li and Pearl, as well as Shu et al., extended PoCs to multivalued settings and derived corresponding theoretical bounds. These developments naturally raise the question of whether additional causal knowledge can further tighten the bounds in multivalued settings. This paper addresses this question by deriving tighter bounds for multivalued PoCs through the incorporation of causal information encoded in covariates and mediators. We illustrate the theoretical results with toy examples, while simulation studies further demonstrate that the proposed bounds are tighter than existing nonbinary bounds.

</details>


### [173] [On the Expressive Power of Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12671)
*Phokion Kolaitis,Rik Sengupta*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文综述了利用电路复杂性理论刻画Transformer表达力的研究成果，系统比较了Transformer与标准计算模型在语言识别能力上的差异，并总结了不同资源约束下的精确边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多层Transformer已成为所有大语言模型的核心组件，其普遍性与计算能力催生了大量旨在精确校准其作为语言识别器表达能力的研究。通过将其与理论计算机科学中经典计算模型对比，可深入理解其本质能力。电路复杂性因能自然对应Transformer的注意力与精度等资源参数，成为分析其表达力的最合适框架。

Method: 采用电路复杂性作为核心分析工具，将Transformer的资源（如注意力头数、精度、层数）映射到电路参数（如门类型、规模、深度），从而建立Transformer与不同电路复杂性类别之间的直接对应关系，并通过综述已选结果来系统梳理边界。

Result: 展示了系列具体定理与分类结果，明确界定了不同资源约束下Transformer所能识别的语言类别，揭示了其在电路复杂性层级中的精确位置，并指出了与标准计算模型（如正则语言、上下文无关语言等）的异同。

Conclusion: 电路复杂性为分析Transformer表达力提供了严谨且有效的理论框架，已有结果勾勒出其能力边界，但仍有诸多开放问题待解，未来研究可继续深化这一对应关系并拓展至更复杂的模型变体。

Abstract: Multi-layer transformers form the critical component of essentially all large language models (LLMs) in use today. Because of their ubiquity and computational capability, there is a rapidly growing body of work that aims to precisely calibrate the expressive power of transformers as language recognizers by comparing them against standard models of computation studied for decades by the theoretical computer science community. In this endeavor, circuit complexity has by and large emerged as the "correct" branch of computational complexity to analyze the expressive power of transformers; the reason is that parameterizing transformers by the various resources they use, such as attention and precision, leads to direct comparisons with different classes of circuits parameterized by resources such as type of gates, size, and depth. Here, we present an overview of selected results that delineate the expressive power of transformers using concepts and methods from circuit complexity.

</details>


### [174] [Federated Compositional Muon Optimizer for Matrix-Wise Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12710)
*Wang Yan,Feihu Huang*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出FedCoMuon及其方差缩减变体FedCoMuon-VR，用于求解分布式矩阵级组合优化问题，在非凸非独立同分布下达到O(ε⁻³)样本复杂度，并在联邦稳健学习和风险敏感元学习任务中优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Muon优化器虽在矩阵级模型中有效，但其变体对层次化结构问题（如联邦组合优化）适配不足，缺乏针对分布式组合目标的专门设计，导致收敛性和样本效率受限。

Method: 基于组合梯度跟踪与正交化动量构建FedCoMuon，并通过动量方差缩减技术扩展出FedCoMuon-VR，以降低随机噪声并加速收敛。理论分析覆盖非独立同分布与非凸设定。

Result: FedCoMuon-VR达到O(ε⁻³)的样本复杂度，低于现有FedMuon算法；实验在多个联邦学习与元学习任务上取得最佳准确率，且与组合基线竞争。

Conclusion: FedCoMuon系列有效填补Muon在层次化分布式组合问题上的空白，兼具理论保证与实证优势，为联邦组合优化提供新方案。

Abstract: Muon, a more recently developed optimizer, is useful for matrix-wise models in AI areas. Although many works have studied Muon and its variants, these methods are still not particularly well-suited for hierarchical structured problems. To fill this gap, we propose an effective federated compositional Muon (FedCoMuon) optimizer to solve distributed matrix-wise compositional optimization problems. Specifically, our FedCoMuon optimizer builds on compositional gradient tracking and orthogonalized momentum. Moreover, we propose a variance reduced variant of FedCoMuon (FedCoMuon-VR) based on a momentum-based variance reduced technique. In theory, we analyze the convergence properties of our algorithms under the non-i.i.d. and non-convex settings. In particular, we prove that our FedCoMuon-VR obtains a lower sample complexity of $O(ε^{-3})$ for finding an $ε$-stationary solution than the existing FedMuon algorithms. Extensive numerical experiments on robust federated learning and task-distributed risk-sensitive meta learning show that our proposed methods are competitive with existing compositional baselines and achieve the best reported accuracy in several settings.

</details>


### [175] [Lines and Ladders: A Context-Aware Multi-Agent Framework for Large-Scale Retail Price Taxonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12674)
*Ravi Teja Chunduri,Srikaran Reddy Boya,Deep Narayan Mishra,Ajay Kumar B,Karthik Kumaran,Pranay Kona*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出一个可扩展的多智能体框架，用于自动化构建零售定价分类体系（Lines and Ladders），在真实企业数据上取得优于单智能体的效果并已部署生产。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球零售商需维持价格一致性和每日低价策略，但数百万活跃商品的人工价格治理不可行；变体间定价不一致会扭曲客户价值感知并蚕食销量，亟需自动化方法构建定价分类体系。

Method: 采用专门化的LLM智能体框架，通过识别关键属性、提取多模态值并应用层次化分组逻辑来构建定价结构；以3智能体系统分工协作，缓解单智能体的认知过载问题。

Result: 3智能体系统在线（Lines）任务上F1分数达0.83，优于单智能体基线；在食品与消耗品类目实现>90%精确率和>75%召回率，在非结构化综合商品目录中分配准确率达80.2%。

Conclusion: 该多智能体框架能有效自动化构建定价分类体系，在真实生产环境中表现稳健，可扩展至大规模目录并改善价格治理效率。

Abstract: Maintaining price consistency and executing an Every Day Low Price strategy is critical for global retailers. However, with catalogs spanning millions of active items, manual governance of price relationships is infeasible. Inconsistent pricing across item variants distorts customer value perception and cannibalizes sales. To address this, we present a scalable, context-aware Multi-Agent Framework designed to automate the construction of "Lines and Ladders" pricing taxonomies. Our framework employs specialized LLM agents to construct these coherent pricing structures by identifying key attributes, extracting multi-modal values, and applying hierarchical grouping logic. Evaluated on real-world enterprise data and deployed in production, our 3-Agent system achieves an F1-score of 0.83 for Lines, outperforming single-agent baselines by mitigating cognitive overload. The system achieves >90% precision and >75% recall in Food & Consumables, and 80.2% assignment accuracy in the unstructured General Merchandise catalog.

</details>


### [176] [Perturbation-based Regional Interpretability through Subtraction Mapping (PRISM): naming-error dissociations in language models and post-stroke aphasia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12717)
*Xiang Guan,Roger D. Newman-Norlund,Yong Yang,Saeed Ahmadi,Regan Willis,Nadra Salman,Kalil Warren,Srihari Nelakuditi,Chris Rorden,Leonardo Bonilha,Julius Fridriksson*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: PRISM将人类神经影像学的减法分析框架适配到Transformer大模型中，通过逐层扰动和配对误差类减法，在LLM与失语症患者之间实现了空间解析、可证伪的功能特化检验，并在两个基质上复现了音位优势的解离模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的机制可解释性缺乏空间解析且可证伪的工具来检验内部组件是否专用于不同的认知操作，而人类神经影像学中的减法分析为此提供了成熟范式。

Method: 基于BLUM框架，PRISM对LLaVA-1.6-Vicuna-13B进行逐层扰动，配对减去七类费城命名测试错误类别，将每个扰动种子视为一个分析主体，沿层轴使用阈值自由簇增强进行组分析；同时对213例慢性卒中后失语症患者进行相关性差异病变-症状映射的平行分析，两者在主体、空间和阈值维度上匹配，但对比算子不同（LLM为被试内错误比例差，皮层为被试间相关差）。

Result: 两个基质均稳健恢复音位偏好解离，包括深层簇（LLM）和额叶-外侧裂周皮层簇（患者），且均通过保留集复制；语义偏好方向在两边均表现为符号一致但不显著的趋势。

Conclusion: PRISM为Transformer语言模型的功能特化主张提供了可证伪、空间解析的检验，而确认性的ROI级因果干预（PRISM第三阶段）留待后续工作。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability of large language models lacks spatially resolved, falsifiable tools for testing whether internal components are specialized for distinct cognitive operations. We adapt subtraction analysis, the standard framework of human neuroimaging, from biological brains to perturbed transformers, and apply the same logic to both substrates in parallel. Building on the Brain-LLM Unified Model (BLUM), which showed that layer-perturbed LLaVA-1.6-Vicuna-13B error profiles match the lesion patterns of aphasic patients, we develop PRISM (Perturbation-based Regional Interpretability through Subtraction Mapping). PRISM maps the seven clinical Philadelphia Naming Test categories, subtracts error classes pairwise, and treats each perturbation seed as a subject in a group analysis with threshold-free cluster enhancement along the layer axis. We run a structurally matched analysis on 213 chronic post-stroke aphasia patients using correlation-difference lesion-symptom mapping, and replicate both sides on held-out splits. The designs match in subject dimension (seeds, patients), spatial dimension (layers, atlas-parcellated cortex) and thresholding, but the contrast operator differs: a within-subject error-proportion difference for the LLM, a between-subject correlation difference for the cortex. Both substrates recover a robust phonemic-favoring dissociation, a deep layer cluster and a frontal-perisylvian cortical cluster, both replicating; the semantic-favoring direction is a consistently signed but non-significant trend on both. PRISM thus gives a falsifiable, spatially resolved test of functional-specialization claims in transformer language models. A confirmatory ROI-level intervention (PRISM Stage 3) licensing the strongest causal-mechanism claim is left to subsequent work.

</details>


### [177] [PROVE-RT: Generating Mechanized Theorem Prover Scripts for Real-Time Systems using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12762)
*Sadat Shahriyar,Shareef Ahmed,Abdullah Al Arafat*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出PROVE-RT框架，通过依赖感知草图、文档检索、分阶段骨架生成和证明补全，利用LLM自动生成PROSA/ROCQ脚本，将调度性分析机械化成功率提升至44.7%，远超直接提示LLM的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有调度性分析测试依赖手工证明，难以扩展和验证；机械化验证（如PROSA/ROCQ）虽可靠但手工构建证明成本极高，需要领域专家和大量工程投入。LLM虽具潜力，但缺乏PROSA特定知识，无法直接生成正确脚本。

Method: PROVE-RT框架包含四个关键组件：1）依赖感知的非正式草图生成；2）从处理的PROSA文档中检索相关知识；3）分阶段骨架生成以逐步构建证明结构；4）证明完成阶段填充细节。此外，从1,191篇实时系统论文构建了包含13,134个带依赖信息的非正式草图的机械化语料库。

Result: 在精选评估集上，直接提示最新LLM无法可靠生成有效PROSA机械化脚本，而PROVE-RT实现了44.7%的成功率，显著优于基线。

Conclusion: 检索引导和分阶段的LLM辅助方法能有效提升调度性分析的自动化机械化水平，为实时系统认证提供了可扩展的新路径，表明结合领域知识和结构化生成策略是解锁LLM在形式验证领域潜力的关键。

Abstract: Schedulability analysis is essential for certifying real-time systems, but existing tests are often developed through pen-and-paper proofs that are difficult to scale, validate, and maintain. Mechanized verification in PROSA/ROCQ offers a rigorous alternative, yet manually constructing such proofs requires substantial domain expertise and proof-engineering effort. Recent successes of large language models (LLMs) across a wide range of tasks make them promising candidates for generating PROSA/ROCQ scripts for mechanized theorem provers. However, state-of-the-art LLMs often lack the PROSA-specific knowledge required to correctly use its modeling abstractions and proof patterns.
  This paper introduces PROVE-RT, an LLM-assisted framework for generating PROSA/ROCQ scripts to mechanize schedulability analyses in real-time systems literature. PROVE-RT guides generation through dependency-aware informal sketches, retrieval from processed PROSA documentation, staged skeleton generation, and proof completion. We construct a mechanization-oriented corpus from 1, 191 real-time systems papers, containing 13, 134 informal sketches with dependency information. On a curated evaluation set, direct prompting of state-of-the-art LLMs fails to reliably generate valid PROSA mechanizations, whereas PROVE-RT achieves a success rate of 44.7%. These results show that retrieval-guided and staged LLM assistance can improve automated mechanization of schedulability analysis in PROSA/ROCQ.

</details>


### [178] [Fast A/B/n Testing: Exact Multi-Policy Comparison via Tree-Coupled Feedback Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12831)
*Yuxiao Wen*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出Tree-Coupled A/B Testing（TCAB），一种通过树结构耦合策略历史并共享奖励的反馈共享设计，使每策略保持独立轨迹分布的同时，将期望奖励查询次数从JT降至T+o(T)，在保留精确策略推断的前提下显著降低交互成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线平台需比较多个自适应决策策略（如推荐算法、语言模型智能体），但直接A/B/n设计需消耗JT次奖励交互，成本高昂且可能带来风险。现有方法难以在保持策略独立分布（用于有效推断）与共享反馈（降低样本量）之间取得平衡，缺乏一种既精确又高效的多策略比较框架。

Method: TCAB通过每轮构建可预测树连接当前策略历史，对父子上下文-动作分布进行最大耦合，并沿匹配树边共享单个奖励。定义D_e,t为边e在t轮的不匹配指示，路径wise奖励查询数满足N(T)=T+ΣD_e,t（即T加累计树边全变差）。算法选择最小生成树作为当前轮耦合结构，并证明该设计在条件期望下最优。

Result: 理论上：对固定J，若各策略伪遗憾次线性且oracle动作几乎必然唯一，则期望查询次数E[N(T)]=T+o(T)（对比JT）；同时给出成对策略对比的有限样本方差界。实验中：在奖励模型评估、多选语言模型评估和自适应搜索策略上，验证了成本-精度前沿的显著改进。

Conclusion: TCAB首次实现了对任意依赖历史的自适应上下文bandit策略的精确反馈共享，在不改变每策略边际轨迹分布的前提下将查询成本从线性于J降至接近独立于J，提供了理论最优性与实践有效性兼具的解决方案，适用于高成本或高风险的多策略在线比较场景。

Abstract: Online platforms increasingly compare many adaptive decision policies---ranking systems, recommendation algorithms, pricing rules, and language-model agents---while each reward-bearing interaction can be costly or risky. A direct A/B/n design gives each of $J$ policies its own horizon-$T$ trajectory and therefore uses $JT$ outcomes. We introduce Tree-Coupled A/B Testing (\TCAB), an exact feedback-sharing design for arbitrary history-dependent contextual-bandit policies. At each round, a predictable tree connects the current policy histories; every parent--child context--action law is maximally coupled, and one reward is shared within each component of matched tree edges. Every policy retains exactly its standalone finite-horizon trajectory law, even though the policies are deliberately dependent. If $D_{e,t}$ records a mismatch on tree edge $e$ at round $t$, the number of reward queries satisfies the pathwise identity $N(T)=T+\sum_{t,e}D_{e,t}$ and hence equals $T$ plus cumulative tree-edge total variation in expectation. This cost is conditionally optimal among exact edge-local designs on the selected tree, and a current-round minimum-spanning tree is myopically optimal among tree designs. For fixed $J$, sublinear pseudo-regret of every policy and almost-sure uniqueness of the oracle action imply $\mathbb{E}[N(T)]=T+o(T)$, versus $JT$ for independent runs. We also obtain finite-sample variance bounds for pairwise policy contrasts. Experiments on reward-model evaluation, multiple-choice language-model evaluation, and adaptive search policies demonstrate substantial improvements in the cost--precision frontier.

</details>


### [179] [Sustaining Plasticity via Learnable Wavelet Activations in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12874)
*Zeyang Zhang,Tieliang Gong,Junyan Lu,Weizhan Zhang*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出一种可学习的小波激活函数，通过将激活分解为低频和高频成分并结合动态注入与正则化，解决持续学习中的塑性损失问题，在多个基准上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习中塑性损失严重阻碍序列任务获取；固定形式激活存在低频偏置，而可学习变体更新无约束易导致灾难性遗忘，现有方法无法同时解决这两个问题。

Method: 设计可学习小波激活，显式分解低频与高频分量以对抗谱偏置；采用动态小波注入增强新任务塑性，并引入正则化保持旧知识稳定；理论上证明混合小波结构对L2逼近的必要性，推导入射触发机制并验证解耦学习率恢复高频塑性。

Result: 在多个持续学习基准上，方法在训练性和泛化性上均保持优势，达到当前最优水平。

Conclusion: 该框架有效平衡了塑性维持与稳定性，为持续学习提供了一种理论有据且实践高效的激活设计方案。

Abstract: Plasticity loss has emerged as a critical challenge in continual learning that significantly hinders the acquisition of sequential tasks. While optimizing activation designs offers a potential solution, current fixed-form functions suffer from an inherent spectral bias towards low-frequency variations, whereas learnable variants permit unconstrained updates that induce catastrophic forgetting. To address these limitations, we propose a novel learnable wavelet activation that decomposes the activation function into low-frequency and high-frequency components to explicitly counter spectral bias. Furthermore, we employ dynamic wavelet injection to adaptively enhance plasticity for new tasks, alongside a regularization strategy to ensure the stability of previous learned knowledge. Theoretically, we provide rigorous mathematical guarantees for the proposed framework, proving the structural necessity of the hybrid wavelet architecture for efficient $L^2$ approximation and demonstrating that the decoupled learning rate mechanism successfully restores network plasticity for high-frequency information. Additionally, we provide a formal derivation of the loss-driven injection trigger mechanism to precisely guide the injection. Extensive empirical evaluations demonstrate that our approach maintains superior trainability and generalization throughout the learning process and achieves state-of-the-art performance across diverse continual learning benchmarks.

</details>


### [180] [AI and Consumer Rights in India Working Paper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12863)
*Omir Kumar,Sriya Sridhar,Vibhav Mithal,Balaraman Ravindran*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文探讨印度《2019年消费者保护法》能否有效应对AI产品致害责任，指出其技术中立定义虽可覆盖AI损害，但在因果关系证明、价值链责任划分及跨行业执法上存在明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统在消费场景中普及，AI相关损害的归责问题悬而未决，需要评估现有法律框架是否适配AI的独特技术特征和多主体价值链。

Method: 通过法律文本分析，审视该法对产品责任、损害及缺陷的宽泛定义，并结合AI故障特性（如设计选择而非离散缺陷）及价值链多主体重叠责任进行适用性检验。

Result: 该法在形式上可涵盖AI损害（如人身伤害、心理损害、偏见输出等），但存在关键缺口：因果关系举证困难、现有角色分类无法映射AI价值链的复杂协作关系，且缺乏比例化责任分配机制，需明确跨行业监管协调。

Conclusion: 印度现行消费者保护法虽具备技术中立潜力，但需修订以解决AI特有挑战，否则难以公平有效追究多方责任。

Abstract: As AI systems proliferate in consumer facing applications, questions about liability for AI related harms remain unresolved.
