This working paper examines whether India's Consumer Protection Act, 2019, adequately addresses harm caused by defective AI products and services, and whether it proportionately allocates liability across the AI value chain.
  The Act's broad definitions of product liability, harm, and deficiency appear technology agnostic and potentially applicable to AI related incidents including personal injury, psychological harm, biased outputs, and loss of control. However, significant gaps remain. Proving causation between AI defects and consumer harm presents a technical challenge, as AI failures often stem from design choices rather than discrete defects. Additionally, the Act's framework assumes distinct roles for manufacturers, sellers, and service providers, yet the AI value chain involves overlapping responsibilities among data providers, model developers, deployers, and users that do not neatly map to these categories. Current liability frameworks lack proportionate mechanisms to effectively address complex, multistakeholder AI harms. While the Act may cover AI entities, enforcement requires clarification on sector specific overlaps.

</details>


### [181] [Revisiting Overestimation Bias Problem of Q-learning: Settling Large Discrete Action Space via Action Intersection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12912)
*Pu Li,Tao Tan,Hong Xie,Xiaoyu Shi,Mingsheng Shang*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出动作交集策略，通过半解耦设计（共享部分数据时用耦合更新，否则用解耦更新）缓解大动作空间下Q-learning的过估计偏差，实现偏差从欠估计到过估计的连续可调，并在表格和深度强化学习实验中验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在大动作空间下受随机性影响：耦合范式（同一Q函数估计最优动作及其值）产生正偏差，解耦范式（双独立Q函数）产生负偏差，两者均有瓶颈。

Method: 动作交集策略：两个Q函数共享部分轨迹数据，共享样本采用耦合更新，非共享样本采用解耦更新；通过调整共享比例（动作交集大小）可精细控制偏差方向与幅度。

Result: 深度RL实验大幅优于多个SOTA基线；表格实验揭示该方法性能优越的机制：可实现从欠估计到过估计的宽偏差范围及细粒度控制。

Conclusion: 动作交集是一种简单而强大的策略，能有效缓解大动作空间下Q-learning的过估计问题，为偏差控制提供新思路。

Abstract: This paper considers the overestimation bias problem of Q-learning in the setting of a large action space, for the purpose of relieving the bottleneck of existing methods. We find that the large action space increases the randomness in Q-value estimation. The randomness makes two paradigms that drive the major literature on the overestimation problem have their own bottlenecks: the coupling paradigm, i.e., the optimal action and its Q-value are estimated with the same Q-function, always has a positive bias. This is because randomness leads to some actions having abnormally high estimated values than their true values, and the coupling methods prefer these actions. The decoupling paradigm, i.e., the optimal action and its Q-value are estimated with two independent Q-functions, always has a negative bias. This is because randomness increases the estimation gap between the two independent Q-tables for the same action. This paper shows that action intersection can be a simple yet powerful strategy to relieve these bottlenecks. The action intersection strategy enables semi-decoupling via two designs: (1) it allows two Q-functions to share a certain fraction of trajectory data; (2) if a data sample is shared, each Q-function is updated using the coupling paradigm; otherwise, using the decoupling paradigm. Two properties make the action intersection strategy powerful: (1) attaining a large bias range, i.e., varying the data sharing fraction, the estimation bias varies from underestimating to overestimating; (2) fine granularity: the action intersection size can be made arbitrarily finer to enable finer control. We consider two experiment settings, i.e., tabular and deep RL, deep RL experiments show that our method outperforms several SOTA baselines drastically; tabular experiments reveal why our method can achieve superior performance.

</details>


### [182] [Agent Behavioral Contracts II: Certifying Compositional Reliability Without Assuming Independence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12895)
*Varun Pratap Bhardwaj,Garima Singh,Arun Pratap Bhardwaj*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 针对多智能体系统中组件可靠性相乘的独立性假设，通过3万次任务测试验证了其不成立：同模型智能体故障高度正相关，导致冗余可靠性被高估；提出无依赖假设的有限样本线性规划证书和随时有效证书，显著提升可靠性下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统可靠性组合计算依赖条件独立性假设，但该假设极少被检验，一旦失效会错误高估冗余收益。

Method: 通过确定性代码执行1.8万次任务（预注册）检验同模型/异模型故障相关性；证明拟合依赖模型的自举界随样本增大失去覆盖；提出基于Bonferroni-Clopper-Pearson矩盒的线性规划联合分布证书及随时有效变体。

Result: 同模型共故障率高达90%（phi=0.916）；换模型显著降低相关性，换供应商无影响；证书区间随矩功能数从10增至14收窄85.7%，可靠性下界从0.2455升至0.4116；随时证书在可选停止下维持0.0471错误率。

Conclusion: 独立性假设在共享模型时系统性高估冗余可靠性，所提无假设证书虽偏保守但可靠且随信息丰富度改进，为多智能体系统可靠性评估提供安全替代方案。

Abstract: Compositional reliability bounds for multi-agent systems multiply component reliabilities, a step licensed by a conditional-independence assumption that is routinely stated and rarely tested. We test it. Two instances of one model, in a two-agent handoff, co-fail on 90.0% of the missions on which either fails (log OR 6.66, 95% CI [6.38, 7.00]; phi 0.916), in a preregistered evaluation of 18,000 missions scored by deterministic code with no LLM judge. Substituting a different model reduces the association in six of six contrasts; substituting a different vendor, model already different, does not -- a registered hypothesis reported as a null.
  The error is signed and runs against the operator: positive dependence inflates joint failure above the independence product, so redundancy is over-credited exactly when components share a model. The assumption-free alternative is often vacuous, and fitting a dependence model is worse: we prove a bootstrap bound on a fitted model's functional loses coverage of the truth as n grows, the identification gap being O(1) while the bootstrap haircut is O(n^{-1/2}). More data makes such a certificate worse, with no visible symptom.
  We give a finite-sample certificate assuming no dependence structure: a linear program over the joint, over a Bonferroni-Clopper-Pearson box around measured co-execution moments. It is sound, sharp for the information supplied, and monotone in the moment family. Enriching ten moment functionals to fourteen narrows the identified interval by 85.7% and lifts the certified floor from 0.2455 to 0.4116. A companion anytime-valid certificate holds type-I error at 0.0471 under optional stopping.
  Common dependence statistics are marginal-bounded and can reverse an apparent ordering of conditions when the compared agents fail at different rates. Contracts, scoring code, analysis scripts, and the preregistration are released.

</details>


### [183] [Polish Medical Visual Question Answering: Vision-Language Models Underutilize Visual Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12928)
*Jakub Pokrywka,Łukasz Grzybowski,Antoni Lasik,Marek Kubis,Jeremi Ignacy Kaczmarek,Wojciech Kusa*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个波兰语医学视觉问答基准，评估多种视觉语言模型后发现任务仍具挑战性，最佳模型准确率为79.0%，仅GPT-5.6超过人类参考水平，且模型更依赖问题文本而非图像，并在图像主导问题上表现更差，同时能从答案选项中获取非平凡信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学VQA基准主要针对英语，缺乏波兰语等低资源语言的评测资源，导致波兰语医学视觉语言模型的发展缺乏标准评估工具，难以衡量其实际临床辅助能力。

Method: 构建波兰语医学VQA基准，数据源自波兰医师和牙医专科认证考试，包含多专科、多视觉域的图像问题及文本对照集；评估多类模型，并通过消融实验（去除图像、问题或两者）分析视觉接地，按图像重要性对问题分类。

Result: 最佳模型在完整VQA集上准确率为79.0%，仅GPT-5.6在可选答案子集上超过人类参考；其他模型均低于人类。模型从问题文本获取的信息多于图像，在图像主导问题上表现更差；在QA和VQA中，模型仅凭答案选项也能达到高于随机的准确率。

Conclusion: 波兰语医学VQA任务对现有模型仍具显著挑战，尤其图像理解是短板；模型存在依赖文本捷径和答案偏见的问题，未来需改进视觉编码与跨模态推理，并扩展基准覆盖更多语言和医学场景。

Abstract: We introduce a Polish-language medical visual question answering (VQA) benchmark, built from Polish Board Certification Examination questions for licensed physicians and dentists pursuing specialist certification. The benchmark comprises image-containing questions spanning diverse medical specialties and visual domains, together with a text-only question answering (QA) control set. We evaluate Polish-oriented, general-purpose open-weight, and commercial vision-language models. The task remains challenging: the best model achieves 79.0\% accuracy on the full VQA set, and only GPT-5.6 surpasses the approximate human reference on the subset with available candidate responses; all other evaluated models perform worse than humans. To assess visual grounding, we compare complete inputs with configurations omitting the image, the question, or both, and categorize questions by image importance. Models derive more useful information from the question text than from the image and perform worse on image-dominant questions. Across both QA and VQA, they nevertheless achieve above-chance accuracy from the answer choices alone, showing that non-trivial performance can persist even when key task components are missing.

</details>


### [184] [Diagnosing JEPA World Models with Action-Conditioned Predictive Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12939)
*Guo An,Zijing Wu,Honghua Dong,Yuhao Yan,Zixuan Gui,Haochong Chen,Shanzhao Ruan,Xiang Wang,Yurong Ling,Qi Tian*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出一种基于双模拟准则的度量标准 Action-Conditioned Predictive Consistency (ACPC)，用于衡量视觉扰动对联合嵌入预测架构中状态表示和后续预测的影响，并证明其能界定扰动导致的预测误差与规划代价变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合嵌入预测架构（JEPAs）虽在潜空间预测以避开像素级外观建模压力，但无法保证对视觉扰动的鲁棒性，因为扰动仍可能改变编码表示并影响行动条件预测。双模拟准则要求仅在行动条件后果一致时视两个观测为同一状态，为满足此需求，需引入可量化诊断。

Method: 提出 ACPC 作为诊断指标，衡量干净历史与视觉扰动视图在相同动作序列滚动后的发散程度，证明该发散上界可约束多步预测误差及规划代价的变化。进一步构建互补指标：Invariance Radius (IR) 汇总干净-扰动滚动扩散范围，Separation Rate (SR) 检验不同状态滚动后是否仍可区分。

Result: 在四个视觉控制任务上验证 ACPC 可预测扰动引起的预测误差与代价变化。在 LeWM 上，IR-SR 组合可跨任务迁移，且对模糊和缩放扰动仍保持有效性；PLDM 在另一种架构下也呈现相似诊断趋势。

Conclusion: ACPC 及其扩展指标 IR/SR 提供了一种可靠的诊断工具，用于评估和筛选对视觉扰动鲁棒的 JEPA 模型，有助于在模型选择或训练中兼顾预测一致性与状态可区分性。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) learn world models that predict in a compact latent space rather than in pixels, reducing the pressure to model nuisance appearance. Yet this provides no guarantee against visual perturbations: they can still alter the encoded representation and affect subsequent action-conditioned predictions. Bisimulation captures this requirement precisely: two observations should be treated as the same state only when their action-conditioned consequences agree. Guided by this criterion, we introduce Action-Conditioned Predictive Consistency (ACPC), a diagnostic that measures how far a clean history and a visually perturbed view of it diverge after being rolled forward under the same action sequence. We prove that this divergence bounds the perturbation-induced change in multi-step prediction error and planner cost. Building on pairwise ACPC, we define two complementary measures: the Invariance Radius (IR) summarizes clean-perturbed rollout spread, while the Separation Rate (SR) checks whether different states remain distinguishable after rollout. Experiments on four visual control tasks show that pairwise ACPC predicts perturbation-induced prediction and cost changes. On LeWM, the IR-SR screen transfers across tasks, and the joint diagnostic remains informative under blur and resize. PLDM exhibits similar diagnostic trends under a different architecture.

</details>


### [185] [BoardroomAI: Dependency-Aware Human-Steerable Multi-Agent Deliberation through Evolving Decision Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13046)
*Sanjeev Manivannan*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出BoardroomAI框架，将人类作为持续参与者融入多智能体决策过程，通过类型化决策图、干预编译器、依赖感知传播和评估框架，实现选择性修复，在600次干预中仅检查14.59%节点即匹配穷举结果，12例试点中保留所有未受影响节点，但6例因上下文不足而弃权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统转录式多智能体系统中人类仅在初始提供问题，无法在决策过程中动态干预；实际组织决策中证据、约束和优先级持续演变，需要人类与智能体持续协作。

Method: 四个组件：类型化决策图（表示证据、假设、约束等语义关系）、干预编译器（将人类确认操作转为图更新）、依赖感知传播（识别受影响子图并选择性重新激活专家）、评估框架（衡量干预影响、修复覆盖率、保留率、重计算量和决策有效性）。

Result: 600次合成决策DAG干预中，传播方法仅检查14.59%节点即与穷举影响计算一致；12例试点中，选择性修复重算62.11%规范节点，保留所有黄金未影响节点，6例产生有效更新决策、6例弃权。

Conclusion: 正确干预路由可能仍缺乏综合上下文，需引入‘决策充分上下文闭包’；所有结果为合成数据上的原型级验证，表明框架可行但需进一步优化上下文供给。

Abstract: Organizational decisions are co-created while evidence, constraints, and human priorities continue to evolve. In conventional transcript-based multi-agent systems, humans typically provide an initial problem, agents deliberate internally, and the system returns a final response. BoardroomAI instead treats the human as a persistent participant who can intervene by challenging assumptions, modifying constraints, changing priorities, introducing evidence, or redirecting the decision process. We operationalize this human--agent coexistence through four components: (i) a typed decision graph representing evidence, assumptions, constraints, claims, objections, alternatives, risks, decisions, semantic dependencies, and specialist responsibility; (ii) an intervention compiler that converts confirmed human actions into explicit graph updates; (iii) dependency-aware propagation that identifies affected subgraphs, preserves unaffected artifacts, and selectively reactivates relevant specialists; and (iv) an evaluation framework measuring intervention impact, repair coverage, preservation, recomputation, and decision validity. Across 600 generated decision-DAG interventions, propagation matched exhaustive impact computation while inspecting only 14.59% of nodes. In a 12-case exploratory pilot, selective repair recomputed 62.11% of canonical nodes, preserved all gold-unaffected nodes, and produced valid updated decisions in six cases while abstaining in the remaining six. These abstentions show that correct intervention routing may still provide insufficient context for synthesis, motivating a \emph{decision-sufficient context closure} for human-steered multi-agent deliberation. All results are synthetic and prototype-level.

</details>


### [186] [DMDIntel: Interpreting Large Language Models via Dynamic Mode Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13048)
*Amogh Joshi,Animesh Mukherjee,Sergey Utyuzhnikov*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出DMDIntel，利用动态模式分解（DMD）对LLM分类任务的预测进行可解释性分析，通过分解隐藏状态为模式并基于投影值对输入token排序，显著优于现有SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性方法（如PCA、IG、SHAP）在LLM分类任务中难以捕获时序动态特征，导致归因质量有限，亟需更有效的token级归因方法。

Method: 构建输入归因管道：先对LLM隐藏状态进行DMD，提取主导模式；再计算各输入token在模式上的投影值，据此赋予排名分数，形成归因结果。

Result: 在三个数据集和三个模型家族上的广泛实验表明，DMDIntel的token归因排名在效果上大幅超越现有SOTA技术（PCA、IG、SHAP）。

Conclusion: DMDIntel提供了一种基于时序动态分解的高效LLM可解释性工具，为分类任务中的token级归因提供了新的思路，且具有普适性。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce DMDIntel which uses dynamic mode decomposition (DMD) to make the predictions made by LLMs in a classification task interpretable. It develops an input attribution pipeline, that first decomposes the hidden states of an LLM into prominent patterns, also known as modes, and then associates ranks to the input tokens based on the projection values on those modes. Rigorous experiments across three datasets and three model families consistently show that the ranked attribution of input tokens obtained using DMDIntel by far outperforms state-of-the-art techniques such as principal component analysis, integrated gradients and SHAP.

</details>


### [187] [Uniform Herding: Exemplar Replay with Representation Refresh](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13061)
*Krishna Subedi*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出Uniform Herding方法，通过均匀分配活动样本集并刷新代表性样本，在CIFAR-100上相比iCaRL提升最终准确率并显著降低遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特征表示变化时，回放需保留旧类，但活动样本集有限，现有方法（如iCaRL）未考虑在固定预算下随特征变化更新样本。

Method: 将当前活动样本集均匀分配给所有已见类，并利用有界候选池在最新特征表示中刷新每类的代表样本。

Result: 在CIFAR-100十任务增量设置下，Uniform Herding获44.00%±0.51%最终平均准确率，遗忘17.22%±0.43%；iCaRL为42.33%±1.20%和24.87%±1.11%。更换NME或herding会降低准确率，移除蒸馏增加遗忘；检索预算影响小于活动预算。

Conclusion: 该方法在测试协议下优于iCaRL，但比较为端到端，未单独验证刷新效果，结果仅限于所设协议。

Abstract: As the feature representation changes, replay must preserve the earlier classes. However, only a bounded active exemplar set can be replayed. We propose Uniform Herding, which allocates the current active set across observed classes and uses a bounded candidate pool to refresh their chosen exemplars in the current representation. On CIFAR-100 with ten class-incremental tasks, a ResNet-18 backbone, active budget $M=2{,}000$, retrieval budget $b=64$, and three seeds, Uniform Herding obtains $44.00\pm0.51\%$ final average accuracy and $17.22\pm0.43\%$ forgetting, compared with $42.33\pm1.20\%$ and $24.87\pm1.11\%$ for iCaRL. Within the Uniform Herding protocol, final accuracy decreased when NME or herding was replaced with the tested alternatives, while forgetting increased when distillation was removed. Changing the retrieval budget has a smaller effect across the tested range than changing the active budget. The comparison with iCaRL is end-to-end. It does not isolate the effect of refresh from the other protocol differences. These results are limited to the tested protocol.

</details>


### [188] [Explanatory Engagement Under Rare Anomalous Failure: Asymptotic Rarity in Model Behavior (or: The Asymptotic AI)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13063)
*Sam Mao*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 我们研究了LLM在低且可控失败率的工作流中，解释性参与度（长度、特异性、自信度）是否随失败率渐近降低而变化，发现汇总结果呈单调下降，但按条件拆分后，立即强制解释条件支持了参与度上升后平台期而非崩溃的假设，且被动无提示条件下存在模型特定的自我监控差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究关注LLM是否注意到异常，我们则聚焦于模型在低失败率工作流中，当失败率极低时，其解释性参与度是否改变，以揭示异常检测与解释行为之间的动态关系。

Method: 在三个开源模型上执行重复工具调用任务，失败率p从0.2到0.0001扫过八档，结合五种激发条件（从立即提示到无提示），并补充了保证失败的72单元运行以回填零失败样本，分析长度、自信度及自我监控日志。

Result: 汇总结果呈单调下降；立即强制条件下长度在p=0.05时峰值28.4词，随后平台期于17.4-19.0词，自信度从53%升至70s-90s；分组运行无崩溃；被动无提示条件下，llama3.1:8b自发结构化自信报告且有时自我侵蚀，其他模型仅一次性模板化。

Conclusion: 激发结构是崩溃可观测性的首要调节变量；模型在识别异常与识别后参与度上存在差异；未来需细化速率点间行为。

Abstract: Prior work on LLM behavior under anomalous conditions asks whether a model notices anomalies. We ask a narrower question: once a model sits in a workflow with a low, controllable failure rate, does its explanatory engagement - length, specificity, self-reported confidence - change as failure grows asymptotically rarer? We built a local, zero-cost harness on three open-weight models (qwen3:8b, llama3.1:8b, mistral:7b) running a repeated tool-call task where one call fails at probability p, swept across eight rates from 0.2 to 0.0001, under five elicitation conditions from immediate prompting to none. We hypothesized a rise in engagement as failures grew rarer, then a collapse near a detectability threshold. Pooled across conditions this appeared false: length fell in a flat, monotonic pattern. Splitting by condition overturned that. Under immediate_forced, where the model must explain every failure instantly, the predicted rise is confirmed but followed by a plateau, not a collapse: length peaks at 28.4 words at p=0.05, settles to 17.4-19.0 words at the rarest rates, and confidence rises unevenly from about 53% to the 70s-90s. Under grouped_runs, explanation batched to run-end, no collapse appears. Under passive_unprompted, aggregate magnitude is a floor artifact, but a recovered logging gap revealed real, model-specific self-monitoring: llama3.1:8b volunteers structured confidence reports unprompted, sometimes eroding its own confidence as trials accumulate; the other two do so only once, as boilerplate. Elicitation structure is a first-class moderator of collapse observability. A companion guaranteed-failure run (72 cells, backfilling rates where random sampling gave zero real failures) shows models differ in whether they recognize an anomaly, distinct from engagement once recognized. Limitation: discrete rate points cannot capture behavior between them, a direction for future work.

</details>


### [189] [On the global feature importance for interpretable and trustworthy heat demand forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13039)
*Milan Zdravković*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出一种事前可解释AI方法，用于评估区域供暖系统智能控制中热需求预测模型的全属性重要性，旨在提升模型可解释性与可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为应对社区标准合规、客户满意度和责任风险等挑战，需要增强机器学习模型的可解释性和可信度。

Method: 采用四种方法：梯度提升的内在可解释性，以及部分依赖图、累积局部效应和SHAP等事后方法；所有方法均避免特征置换或扰动，以防止引入不真实数据导致的偏差。

Result: 讨论了各方法的互补性，并结合区域供暖过程给出了具体解释。

Conclusion: 所提方法能有效评估全局特征重要性，增强模型透明度，支持区域供暖系统的智能控制。

Abstract: The paper introduces the ante-hoc Explainable AI methodology to assess the global feature importance of the Machine Learning models used for heat demand forecasting in intelligent control of District Heating Systems, with motivation to facilitate their interpretability and trustworthiness, hence addressing the challenges related to adherence to communal standards, customer satisfaction and liability risks. Methodology includes use of four different approaches, namely intrinsic interpretability of Gradient Boosting method and selected post-hoc methods, namely Partial Dependence, Accumulated Local Effects and SHAP. None of the selected methods assume feature permutation or perturbations which can introduce bias due to introduction of random unrealistic values of data instances. Discussion of results is provided, including the assessment of complementarities where applicable, with specific interpretations in context of the district heating processes.

</details>


### [190] [A Multispectral Framework for the Detection of Calcium Carbide-Induced Ripening and Shelf-Life Estimation in Climacteric Fruits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13073)
*Gurbhit Chaurakoti,Harshit Kumar,Hani Kumar,Anurag Singh,Ram Asrey*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出一种基于多光谱成像与XGBoost的非侵入式框架，用于区分自然成熟、乙烯利催熟与碳化钙催熟的水果，并同时估算成熟度和剩余货架期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业级碳化钙非法催熟水果会残留砷和磷，危害健康；现有检测方法多为侵入性或无法同时量化成熟度与货架期，亟需一种快速、非破坏性的检测手段。

Method: 采用AS7265x光谱传感器获取芒果和香蕉在410–940 nm的18个离散波长反射率，结合光谱方差、波长强度比及温湿度特征，经PCA降维（保留>90%方差），训练三个独立的XGBoost模型分别用于分类催熟方式、回归估计成熟度和剩余货架期。

Result: 芒果分类准确率95%（碳化钙类召回率0.67），香蕉分类准确率81%（碳化钙类召回率0.74）；PCA前5–7个主成分保留>90%光谱方差。

Conclusion: 该方法证明非侵入式多光谱结合机器学习可有效识别碳化钙催熟水果，并同步量化品质参数，为食品安全监管提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: Significant health risks are associated with the illegal, yet commonly practiced use of industrial-grade Calcium Carbide (CaC2) for ripening climacteric fruits like mango and banana, which leaves behind trace residues of arsenic and phosphorus. To address this, the proposed study explores a novel, non-invasive multispectral framework for distinguishing safely ripened fruits (naturally ripened and ethephon-induced) from calcium carbide-ripened samples, while also estimating their ripening progression (in percentage) and remaining shelf life (in days). The spectral profiles of mango (Mangifera indica) and banana (Musa acuminata) at 18 discrete wavelengths in the visible-near infrared (NIR) range (410 nm - 940 nm) are studied using the AS7265x spectral triad sensor. CaC2-treated samples exhibit sharper spectral intensity drops in the visible region, consistent with accelerated chlorophyll degradation and carotenoid development. To characterize these physiological changes, the feature engineering strategy integrates inter-method spectral variance, intensity ratios at distinct wavelengths, and environmental parameters including temperature and humidity. Dimensionality reduction using Principal Component Analysis (PCA) retains >90% of spectral variance within the first 5-7 components. The resulting feature set is used to train three independent eXtreme Gradient Boosting (XGBoost)-based learning algorithms for ripening method classification, along with quantitative estimation of remaining shelf life and ripening progression. A classification accuracy of 95% along with carbide class recall of 0.67 is observed for mango samples, while the model achieves an accuracy of 81% and carbide class recall of 0.74 for banana. This instrumentation and data-driven approach demonstrates the effectiveness of the proposed non-invasive framework.

</details>


### [191] [History-informed Lagrangian Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13215)
*Tianshuo Zhang,Xianglei Xing,Wenzhe Zhai,Jia Gao,He Cao*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出HiLNN，利用循环编码器从历史位置序列推断隐速度和系统参数，结合可微RK4滚动优化，在多种物理系统中实现高精度长期预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理引导神经网络如LNN需完整状态输入且难适应参数变化，而实际问题常仅观测位置序列。

Method: HiLNN用循环编码器提取潜在上下文，重建初始速度并调制拉格朗日系统的质量矩阵、势能与阻尼系数，通过可微RK4端到端训练。

Result: 在保守、耗散及变参数系统中，HiLNN的长期预测精度和能量剖面均优于现有基线。

Conclusion: 历史信息编码能有效弥补状态缺失并适应参数变化，为长期动力学预测提供可靠方案。

Abstract: Forecasting the long-horizon evolution of mechanical systems from position-only observations is a pivotal yet difficult task, as hidden velocities and trajectory-specific physical properties must be inferred simultaneously. Although physics-guided neural networks like Lagrangian Neural Networks (LNNs) guarantee physical plausibility, they generally require complete state inputs and lack adaptability to changing system parameters. To break these limitations, we introduce History-informed Lagrangian Neural Networks (HiLNN). Grounded in the insight that temporal position sequences implicitly encode underlying dynamics, HiLNN employs a recurrent encoder to extract a latent context from history. This context not only reconstructs the unobserved initial velocity but also adaptively modulates the mass matrix, potential energy, and damping coefficients of a structured Lagrangian system. By leveraging a differentiable RK4 rollout scheme, the entire pipeline is optimized end-to-end under multi-step trajectory supervision and energy-consistency regularization. Empirical evaluations across conservative, dissipative, and heterogeneous variable-parameter systems show that HiLNN delivers superior long-term prediction accuracy and maintains precise energy profiles compared to state-of-the-art baselines. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/yingtian22/History-informed-LNN.

</details>


### [192] [Knowledge-guided Pattern Discovery via Coupled Tensor Factorizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13234)
*Gaute Johannessen,Geert Roelof van der Ploeg,Evrim Acar*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出一种知识引导的张量分解方法，通过线性耦合联合分析真实数据和计算模型生成的模拟数据，在代谢组学数据中提升模式发现性能并揭示数据与模型间的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂系统数据（如代谢组、脑数据）常为多路张量，现有张量分解主要依赖数据驱动，忽略了计算模型可提供的丰富先验信息，导致模式发现易受噪声影响。

Method: 将真实数据与计算模型生成的模拟数据通过线性耦合张量分解进行联合分析，使分解过程受模拟数据引导，同时保留数据自身的结构。

Result: 在真实代谢组学测量数据上的实验表明，该方法优于纯数据驱动方法，能更准确地发现潜在模式，并有效识别出数据与计算模型之间的不一致性。

Conclusion: 知识引导的张量分解能有效整合数据与计算模型，提高复杂系统模式发现的鲁棒性，并为模型校准或数据修正提供可解释的反馈。

Abstract: In order to understand complex systems such as the human metabolome or human brain, different sensing technologies are used, generating complex data. These datasets are often multiway, i.e., with more than two axes of variation such as a subjects by metabolites by time array. While tensor factorizations have successfully revealed interpretable patterns from such complex data, they have so far been mainly data-driven. On the other hand, there is more to data -- there are computational models (of these systems), which are rich sources of prior information. In this paper, we introduce a knowledge-guided approach that brings together data and computational models by jointly analyzing real data and simulated data (generated using a computational model) using coupled tensor factorizations with linear coupling. Our experiments on real metabolomics measurements demonstrate that guiding the analysis of such noisy data with simulated data improves the pattern discovery performance while also revealing potential discrepancies between data and computational models.

</details>


### [193] [LLM-Guided Graph Generation for Structure-Based Local Improvement Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13333)
*Hai Xia,Vaidyanathan Peruvemba Ramaswamy,Stefan Szeider*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种问题无关的自动流水线，利用LLM生成统一加权图来指导大邻域搜索中的变量选择，在20个MiniZinc问题上实现39.5%的平均加权胜率，较最佳单一配置（19.3%）提升一倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大邻域搜索通常随机选择变量子集，而针对不同问题的变量选择策略依赖领域结构特征，缺乏通用性。

Method: 构建自动流水线，通过语义提示引导LLM为任意MiniZinc问题实例生成均匀加权图（节点为决策变量，边为约束关系），基于该图提取通用特征，用于结构驱动的局部改进框架（SLIM）中的变量选择和配置选择。

Result: 在20个MiniZinc竞赛问题上，算法选择相对一次性Gurobi基线实现39.5%的平均问题加权胜率，超过最佳单一配置（19.3%）；加入配置和特征消融后提升至44.0%。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的语义生成能够实现自动化结构提取和特征提取，有效提升约束优化问题的求解性能，验证了通用图表示和自动化配置选择的可行性。

Abstract: Large neighborhood search normally selects a random subset of decision variables for iterative optimization. For efficiently solving different problems, researchers tend to design variable selection strategies by taking into account structural features from different domains. In this paper, we build an automatic pipeline that is problem-agnostic to all problems in the MiniZinc format. By prompting an LLM with our semantic guidelines, we guide the LLM to produce a graph generator that maps any instance of a problem type to a uniform weighted graph, where nodes represent decision variables and edges represent constraint relationships. These problem-agnostic graphs guide our structure-based local improvement framework (SLIM) in variable selection. Meanwhile, the weighted graph enables all problem instances to share the same generic graph representation, from which the same graph features can be extracted and used for configuration selection. We evaluated our pipeline on instances across 20 MiniZinc competition problems, finding that algorithm selection achieves a 39.5% average problem-weighted win rate against a one-shot Gurobi baseline, more than doubling the best single configuration (19.3%). Configuration and feature ablation boost the performance further to 44.0%, demonstrating that LLM-based semantic generation enables effective automated structure extraction and feature extraction for constraint optimization.

</details>


### [194] [The Time Value of Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13297)
*Matthew Siper,Ahmed Khalifa,Julian Togelius*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出Lineage-Value Policy Gradients (LVPG)框架，在有限预算进化搜索中通过长视界血统价值分配替代即时回报控制，显著提升交易策略发现效率与最终性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 进化搜索中弱子代可能作为高价值祖先，但即时回报控制忽略其延迟效用，导致错失有潜力的进化路径。

Method: 将进化搜索建模为有限时域MDP，LVPG采用共享生成骨干网络，分离actor-critic头：critic头自多步变异树引导估计血统潜力价值，actor头依据剩余预算动态调节变异强度。

Result: 90组配对实验中，路径式信用分配较即时回报优化提升验证最佳AUC 0.394 Sharpe单位，且临时退化更少、恢复更频繁。

Conclusion: 有限时域血统价值在相同资源约束下产生更具选择性的非单调搜索策略，验证了长视界信用分配在进化搜索中的有效性。

Abstract: In evolutionary search, a weak child can be a valuable ancestor that makes high-fitness regions reachable. Immediate-return control is blind to this delayed utility, penalizing mutations through their immediate offspring even when they open productive future lineages. We formalize this hidden dynamic as the time value of evolution within a finite-horizon Markov decision process. To exploit it, we introduce Lineage-Value Policy Gradients (LVPG), a long-horizon actor-critic framework for automated trading policy discovery. Our architecture decouples search control into specialized policy heads over a shared generative backbone: a bootstrapped critic head estimates the value of finite-horizon lineage potential from multi-step mutation trees, while an actor head dynamically modulates mutation intensity over the remaining search budget. We isolate the impact of long-horizon credit assignment against immediate-return optimization across 90 paired runs under matched operators, lineage supervision, folds, seeds, and budgets. Path-based credit assignment substantially accelerates finite-budget search, increasing validation best-so-far AUC by 0.394 Sharpe units. LVPG also produces fewer temporary regressions than immediate-return optimization and recovers from them more often. Finite-horizon lineage value yields more selective non-monotonic search and stronger policies within identical resource constraints.

</details>


### [195] [Where You Measure Decides What You Measure: Position Selection in Ablation-Based SAE Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13337)
*Valentin Noël*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 稀疏自编码器的因果评估中，潜变量测量位置的选择（通常为最大激活 token）由字典本身决定而非实验者，导致不同字典在相同潜变量上常于不同位置比较，产生显著方差；固定测量位置后，跨字典差异大幅下降，且该问题随数据规模增大而恶化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏自编码器评估协议未报告潜变量测量位置，而该位置由被评估字典隐式决定，可能使比较结果反映位置差异而非潜变量本身，威胁评估的有效性与可比性。

Method: 通过比较 Google 发布的两个稀疏自编码器，匹配解码器相似的潜变量对并检查其测量 token；训练六个同一初始化、仅拟合选项不同的自编码器，固定测量位置后重新计算方差；在十六倍语料规模范围内测试位置一致性与规模的关系；提出基于消融的因果指标并审计五篇已发表论文。

Result: 两个字典编码几乎相同的潜变量对中，大量对选择不同的测量 token；固定测量位置后，字典间方差从 7.6% 和 11.9% 降至接近零；语料规模增十六倍时，位置一致性下降而非上升。

Conclusion: 因果评估数值必须附带测量位置报告才能跨论文比较；否则数值同时描述 token 与潜变量，混淆两者贡献。修正仅需一行评估代码，但当前协议普遍缺失该信息。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders are meant to name the things a language model computes, and the usual way to check that a latent matters is to switch it off and see what changes. But a latent fires at many tokens, and the effect has to be measured at one of them. The convention is to measure where the latent fires hardest. That choice is almost never reported, and it is not made by the experimenter: it is made by the dictionary under evaluation. Change the dictionary and the measurement moves to a different token. We show this is not a detail. Take two sparse autoencoders released by Google for the same model and match their latents by decoder similarity: even among the pairs the two dictionaries encode almost identically, they pick different tokens for a large share of them. Two dictionaries compared under the usual protocol are therefore very often compared at different places. To separate the convention from the dictionaries we train six autoencoders from one initialisation, differing only in fitting choices, so that a latent means the same thing in each. Most of the variance such a comparison reads as "these dictionaries disagree about this latent" turns out to be the position instead: it falls from 7.6% and 11.9% of variance to near zero once every dictionary is measured at the same token. More evaluation data does not rescue it. Across a sixteenfold range of corpus sizes the dictionaries agree less about where to measure, not more, so the problem grows with scale. The correction is one line of evaluation code. We give the protocol an ablation-based causal number must report to be comparable across papers, and an audit of five published papers against it. In short: a causal number reported without its position describes the token it was taken at as much as the latent it was taken from.

</details>


### [196] [Academic League of Artificial Intelligence - An Integrative Perspective of Teaching, Research, and Extension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13447)
*Alison R. Panisson,Maria Eduarda W. M. Vianna,Italo Firmino da Silva,Heitor Henrique da Silva,Rafaela Fernandes Savaris,Bernardo Pandolfi Costa,Martin Augusto Gagliotti Vigil,Jim Lau,Agenor Hentz,Andréa Sabedra Bordin,Alexandre Leopoldo Gonçalves,Roberto Rodrigues-Filho*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文介绍了巴西圣卡塔琳娜联邦大学人工智能学术联盟（LIA）的组织框架，该框架以学生为中心、项目为导向，通过民主治理、协作学习和动态项目组织，将教学、科研与大学扩展活动整合于一体，并展示了竞赛团队、学习小组、公开讲座、知识库及社会影响AI应用等代表性举措，证明其是一种灵活可复制的大学三大支柱整合模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术联盟已成为促进课外教育及加强大学与社会联系的重要机制，但现有文献缺乏对其组织框架的系统性描述，尤其缺少如何在同一结构内整合教学、研究与扩展活动的具体实践指南。

Method: 采用案例研究法，基于LIA在UFSC的实际运行经验，提出一个结合民主治理、协作学习与动态项目组织的学生中心化框架，并通过多个代表性项目（如竞赛团队、学习小组、公开讲座、知识库、社会影响AI应用）进行例证说明。

Result: 该框架成功支撑了多样化的教育、科研与扩展活动，促进了领导力、科学产出、社区参与和知识保存，验证了其在统一组织结构下整合三大大学支柱的可行性与有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架为工程与计算教育中整合教学、研究和扩展提供了一种灵活、可复制的模型，可为学术联盟及类似学生组织提供实践指导，并具有推广价值。

Abstract: Academic leagues have become important mechanisms for promoting extracurricular education and strengthening the integration between universities and society. This paper presents the organizational framework adopted by the Academic League of Artificial Intelligence (LIA) at the Federal University of Santa Catarina (UFSC), designed to integrate teaching, research, and university extension through a student-centered, project-based approach. The framework combines democratic governance, collaborative learning, and dynamic project organization to foster both technical and transversal competencies. The framework is illustrated through representative initiatives, including competition teams, study groups, open lectures, knowledge repositories, and AI-powered applications with social impact. These projects demonstrate how diverse educational, scientific, and extension activities can be developed within a common organizational structure while promoting leadership, scientific production, community engagement, and knowledge preservation. The reported experience indicates that the proposed framework provides a flexible and replicable model for integrating the three university pillars into engineering and computing education, offering practical guidance for academic leagues and similar student organizations.

</details>


### [197] [Active-Trace Complexity Bounds for Moreau--Yosida Unadjusted Langevin Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13467)
*Yuchen Xin,Zhihua Zhang*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文分析了MYULA算法在非光滑复合目标上的离散误差，提出用参考活性迹B_ref替代全局曲率界d/λ来控制误差，从而在结构化惩罚下将精度依赖从Õ(ε^{-3})提升到Õ(ε^{-2})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MYULA分析依赖全局曲率上界d/λ导致精度依赖为ε^{-3}，过于保守。实际中非光滑项（如lasso、TV）的Moreau包络局部曲率远小于全局界，需要更精细的误差控制方法。

Method: 通过引入参考活性迹B_ref（MYULA一步热子步沿π_λ的平均Hessian迹）和逐点曲率上界M_λ，结合曲率管估计（curvature-tube）对特定结构化惩罚推导λ无关的B_ref界，并利用Moreau偏置界G^2λ/4平衡步长选择。

Result: 得到迭代复杂度N ≲ (1/m)[L_f + (τ_f+G^2+B_ref)/ε_alg^2 + M_λ/ε_alg]（忽略对数因子），且B_ref≤d/λ给出Õ(ε^{-3})；对lasso型、组稀疏、全变差惩罚，B_ref与λ无关，得到Õ(ε^{-2})。同时证明Moreau偏置界√m W_2(π_λ,π)≤G^2λ/4。

Conclusion: 研究表明经典MYULA核在结构化非光滑问题上的实际表现优于理论预期，其离散误差由数据依赖的局部曲率而非保守全局界主导，为实现端到端O(ε^{-2})精度提供了新理论保证。

Abstract: We study the Moreau--Yosida unadjusted Langevin algorithm (MYULA) for the nonsmooth composite target \[ π(dx)\propto \exp\{-f(x)-g(x)\}\,dx, \qquad x\in\mathbb R^d, \] where \(f\) is \(m\)-strongly convex with \(L_f\)-Lipschitz gradient and \(g\) is convex and \(G\)-Lipschitz. Let \(g_λ\) be the Moreau envelope of \(g\), \(π_λ\) the corresponding smoothed target, and \(a_λ=\operatorname{tr}H_λ\), where \(H_λ\) is the a.e./weak Hessian of \(g_λ\). We show that the leading MYULA discretization error is controlled by the reference active trace \(B_{\mathrm{ref}}\), the average of \(a_λ\) along the heat substep of one MYULA update started from \(π_λ\), rather than by the global curvature bound \(d/λ\). If \(M_λ\) is an a.e. upper bound for \(a_λ\), then, up to logarithmic factors, \[ N \lesssim \frac{1}{m} \left[ L_f + \frac{ τ_f+G^2+B_{\mathrm{ref}} }{ \varepsilon_{\mathrm{alg}}^2 } + \frac{M_λ}{\varepsilon_{\mathrm{alg}}} \right], \qquad τ_f:= \sup_x\operatorname{tr}\nabla^2 f(x), \] iterations suffice to ensure \(\sqrt m\,W_2(μ_N,π_λ)\leq\varepsilon_{\mathrm{alg}}\), where \(μ_N\) is the law of the \(N\)-th iterate and \(W_2\) is the quadratic Wasserstein distance. We also prove the Moreau-bias bound \[ \sqrt m\,W_2(π_λ,π) \leq \frac{G^2λ}{4}. \] Thus, choosing \(λ\asymp\varepsilon/G^2\) gives an end-to-end guarantee for \(π\). The universal estimate \(B_{\mathrm{ref}}\leq d/λ\) yields \(\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-3})\) accuracy dependence. For the structured piecewise-linear, lasso-type, group, and total-variation penalties considered here, curvature--tube estimates make \(B_{\mathrm{ref}}\) independent of \(λ\), yielding \(\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-2})\) for the same classical MYULA kernel.

</details>


### [198] [The data geometry of masking diffusion: Certified-optimal schedules via unmasking growth complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13520)
*Martin J. Wainwright*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出路径分辨的数据几何度量UGC，统一分析离散掩码扩散的KL离散化误差，并据此设计可估计的最优调度采样器，实现常数因子内的迭代复杂度保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散扩散分析缺乏对数据几何的路径级刻画，导致调度与误差界不紧，且无法自适应计算资源。

Method: 定义UGC及其局部增量，证明其控制KL误差；在log-reveal-odds坐标下优化调度；通过耦合揭示轨迹的KL增量估计UGC，构造certified-optimal采样器。

Result: 实现高概率KL误差保证与最优常数迭代复杂度；细划分极限下误差由sqrt(UGC)密度的平方积分决定；示例显示常数块数即可获得Ω̃(√d)维度增益。

Conclusion: UGC为离散扩散提供统一几何框架，支持自适应最优采样，并揭示与经典依赖测度及复杂度测度的深层联系。

Abstract: We study masking diffusion for discrete sampling and introduce a path-resolved measure of data geometry called the \emph{unmasking growth complexity} ({\textsf{UGC}\xspace}). Its local increments directly control Kullback--Leibler (KL) discretization error, yielding a unified analysis of Bernoulli-subset and fixed-cardinality unmasking schemes. In log-reveal-odds coordinates, this structure yields optimized single-block and multi-block schedules, and quantifies the gains from adapting computational effort to data geometry. Crucially, we show how {\textsf{UGC}\xspace} increments can be estimated from samples via KL increments along coupled reveal trajectories. This leads to \emph{certified-optimal} samplers that achieve a prescribed KL error with high probability and iteration complexity within a constant factor of the corresponding oracle procedure. Collapsing the \ugc path yields the aggregate {\textsf{UGC}\xspace} mass, which connects to classical multivariate dependence measures and complexity measures from previous analyses of discrete diffusion. In the fine-partition limit, the squared integral of the square-root {\textsf{UGC}\xspace} density determines the sharp leading-order optimal Euler discretization error. Examples exhibit substantial dimension-dependent gains over coarse schedules, including $\widetildeΩ(\sqrt{d})$ improvements achievable with a constant number of adaptively placed blocks.

</details>


### [199] [Defensive Boosting for Online Probabilistic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13554)
*Georgy Noarov,Aaron Roth*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出防御式提升算法，同时获得在线梯度提升的Brier分数竞争保证与在线分类提升的弱学习条件误差收敛保证，且仅需一个弱学习器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线提升方法只能分别提供两种不兼容的保证：在线梯度提升在Brier分数上与假设类张成空间竞争但在弱学习条件失败时无保证；在线弱到强提升在弱学习条件下驱动误差为零但条件不满足时性能无保证。

Method: 通过操作化提升的'对偶视角'：当随机分类误差持续偏高时，错误权重形成光滑重加权使所有弱假设边缘低，从而提供事后硬核证书表明弱学习条件不成立。算法仅访问单个弱类学习器，并扩展出强自适应变体以在任意时间区间满足保证。

Result: 在合成和真实数据流上，防御式提升器在Brier分数上显著优于所有先前基线（有时大幅提升），且运行时间快数个数量级。

Conclusion: 证明了防御式预测能统一两类在线提升保证，同时实现高效计算与强预测性能，为在线概率预测提供了一种简单且实用的统一框架。

Abstract: We study online probabilistic forecasting of binary outcomes chosen by an adaptive adversary. Given an online learning algorithm for a weak hypothesis class $H$, we would like to efficiently obtain two incomparable guarantees that existing online boosting techniques provide separately. Online gradient boosting competes in Brier score with the best predictor induced by the span of $H$ on every sequence, but promises nothing when the span does not contain an accurate predictor. Online weak-to-strong boosting drives classification error to zero under a weak-learning condition, but promises little when that condition fails.
  We give a simple defensive forecasting algorithm, the Defensive Booster, that obtains both guarantees. On every adaptive sequence, its Brier score is competitive with the best prediction induced by the span of $H$ at the same rate as online gradient boosting; simultaneously, whenever the realized transcript satisfies the smooth weak-learning condition, its Brier score and randomized classification error satisfy the same rate guarantee as online classification boosting. This is achieved by operationalizing the "dual view" of boosting: When the algorithm's randomized classification error is persistently high, its mistake weights form a smooth reweighting on which every weak hypothesis has low edge, yielding an ex-post hard-core certificate that the weak-learning condition fails. We also develop a strongly adaptive variant, which satisfies both guarantees on every time interval. The Defensive Booster is very efficient: it accesses just one weak-class learner, whereas the prior online boosting methods we compare against maintain large weak-learner ensembles. Experiments on synthetic and real data streams demonstrate its strong predictive performance (sometimes substantially improving over all prior baselines) coupled with orders-of-magnitude faster runtime.

</details>
